*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&  Include           ZHUIM_UPDATE3
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extension to ZHUIM_UPDATE solely for size reasons
*
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_SM_PAYMENTS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_sm_payments.

  DATA: l_usrid         TYPE pa0105-usrid.

  REFRESH gt_outtab39.
  SELECT * FROM pa0014
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab39
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND ( subty  = '3500'
            OR   subty  = '3501' ).

  l_usrid = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  SHIFT l_usrid LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
* CONCATENATE 'N' l_usrid INTO l_usrid.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0105
         WHERE usrty = 'FREM'
           AND usrid = l_usrid.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SELECT * FROM pa0014
       APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab39
       WHERE pernr    = pa0105-pernr
         AND ( subty  = '3500'
          OR   subty  = '3501' ).
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR t512t.
  SORT gt_outtab39 BY subty.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab39 INTO gr_outtab39.
    IF gr_outtab39-subty NE t512t-lgart.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t512t
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND molga = '13'
               AND lgart = gr_outtab39-subty.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t512t.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab39-lgtxt = t512t-lgtxt.
    MODIFY gt_outtab39 FROM gr_outtab39.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab39 BY begda DESCENDING
                      subty.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_SM_PAYMENTS

         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr

ENDFORM.                    " GET_EPSS_PERIOD2_RET_2_DUTY

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_EPSS_OTHER_EXT_EARNINGS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_epss_other_ext_earnings.

  DATA: l_dd07v               TYPE dd07v,
        l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
        tb_dd07v              TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF dd07v.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_outtab55> TYPE gs_outtab_type55.
  REFRESH gt_outtab55.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_epss_earn
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab55
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SORT gt_outtab55 BY seq_no.

    REFRESH tb_dd07v.
    SELECT * FROM dd07v
           INTO TABLE tb_dd07v
           WHERE domname = 'ZDO_EARN_TYPE'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab55 ASSIGNING <fs_outtab55>.

        <fs_outtab55>-imp_icon  = icon_import.

        CONCATENATE 'OE' <fs_outtab55>-earning_type <fs_outtab55>-seq_no
                    INTO l_doc_unique.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
               WHERE pernr      = <fs_outtab55>-pernr
                 AND incident   = <fs_outtab55>-incident
                 AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <fs_outtab55>-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
          <fs_outtab55>-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
        ENDIF.

        READ TABLE tb_dd07v INTO l_dd07v WITH KEY domvalue_l = <fs_outtab55>-earning_type.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <fs_outtab55>-ddtext = l_dd07v-ddtext.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_EPSS_OTHER_EXT_EARNINGS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  calculate_ir
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM calculate_ir USING p_selected_date TYPE datum.

  DATA: lv_cpi_rate TYPE /alg/gentk_4decneg_clr.
  DATA: lt_t511k TYPE TABLE OF t511k.
  DATA: ls_t511k TYPE t511k.
  DATA lv_begda_tmp TYPE begda.
  DATA lv_start_date_p2 TYPE begda.
  DATA lv_end_date_p2 TYPE endda.
  DATA lv_selected_date_tmp TYPE begda.
  DATA lv_wkly_75_ir TYPE zwc_normal_base_pay.
  DATA lv_hrly_75_ir TYPE zwc_normal_base_pay.
  DATA lv_frly_75_ir TYPE zwc_normal_base_pay.
  DATA lv_wkly_epss_cap_ir TYPE abrwt.
  DATA lv_yr TYPE char04.

  CLEAR: lt_t511k[].

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2 IS INITIAL.
    lv_start_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_init_start_date_p2.
    lv_end_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_init_end_date_p2.
  ELSE.
    lv_start_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2.
    lv_end_date_p2 = zim_in_cm-epss_end_date_p2.
  ENDIF.

  IF lv_start_date_p2+04(4) GT '1001'.
    lv_yr = lv_start_date_p2(04) + 1.
    CONCATENATE lv_yr '1001' INTO lv_begda_tmp.
  ELSE.
    CONCATENATE lv_start_date_p2(04) '1001' INTO lv_begda_tmp.
  ENDIF.

  IF p_selected_date+04(4) GT '1001'.
    lv_yr = p_selected_date(04) + 1.
    CONCATENATE lv_yr '1001' INTO lv_selected_date_tmp.
  ELSE.
    CONCATENATE p_selected_date(04) '1001' INTO lv_selected_date_tmp.
  ENDIF.

  " Read applicable CPI indexation rates for the interval
  lt_t511k  = cl_hr_t511k=>read_interval(
      molga = '13'
      konst = 'ZECPI'
      begda = lv_begda_tmp
      endda = lv_end_date_p2
  ).
  SORT lt_t511k BY begda.
  LOOP AT lt_t511k INTO ls_t511k.

    lv_cpi_rate = ( ls_t511k-kwert / 100 ) + 1.

    IF lv_wkly_75_ir IS INITIAL.
      lv_wkly_75_ir = w_epss_det-wkly_75 * lv_cpi_rate.
      lv_frly_75_ir = lv_wkly_75_ir * 2.
      lv_hrly_75_ir = lv_frly_75_ir / w_epss_det-plan_hours_p2.
      lv_wkly_epss_cap_ir = w_epss_det-wkly_epss_cap * lv_cpi_rate.
    ELSE.
      lv_wkly_75_ir = lv_wkly_75_ir * lv_cpi_rate.
      lv_frly_75_ir = lv_wkly_75_ir * 2.
      lv_hrly_75_ir = lv_frly_75_ir / w_epss_det-plan_hours_p2.
      lv_wkly_epss_cap_ir = lv_wkly_epss_cap_ir * lv_cpi_rate.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_selected_date_tmp EQ ls_t511k-begda.

      MOVE ls_t511k-kwert TO w_epss_det-ir.
      IF w_epss_det-ir_date IS INITIAL.
        w_epss_det-ir_date = lv_selected_date_tmp.
      ENDIF.
      w_epss_det-wkly_75_ir = lv_wkly_75_ir.
      w_epss_det-hrly_75_ir = lv_hrly_75_ir.
      w_epss_det-wkly_epss_cap_ir = lv_wkly_epss_cap_ir.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM. " calculate_ir
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_RTWP
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_rtwp .

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_rtwp-category,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_first(1)         TYPE c.

  DATA: tb_zim_rtwp_grade TYPE zim_rtwp_grade
                           OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.

  REFRESH gt_outtab20.
  SELECT * FROM zim_rtwp
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab20
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr.

  SORT gt_outtab20 BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab20 INTO gr_outtab20.
    IF gr_outtab20-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab20-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_RTWP_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab20-cat_desc = l_desc.

    IF w_3100_action = 'DISP' OR
       w_3300_action = 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab20-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab20-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_4001_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
        gr_outtab20-upd_icon = icon_display.
      ELSE.
        gr_outtab20-upd_icon = icon_display.
*        gr_outtab20-upd_icon = icon_change.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    gr_outtab20-copy_icon = icon_create_copy.
    gr_outtab20-email_icon = icon_mail.
    IF gr_outtab20-category = 'P' OR
       gr_outtab20-category = 'H'.
      gr_outtab20-conv_icon = icon_convert_all.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab20-category = 'G' OR
       gr_outtab20-category = 'H'.
      CLEAR : gr_outtab20-date_from, gr_outtab20-date_to.
      SELECT * FROM zim_rtwp_grade
           INTO TABLE tb_zim_rtwp_grade
           WHERE pernr     = gr_outtab20-pernr
             AND seq_no    = gr_outtab20-seq_no.
      SORT tb_zim_rtwp_grade BY date_from.
      l_first = abap_true.
      LOOP AT tb_zim_rtwp_grade.
        IF l_first = abap_true.
          gr_outtab20-date_from = tb_zim_rtwp_grade-date_from.
          CLEAR l_first.
        ENDIF.
        gr_outtab20-date_to   = tb_zim_rtwp_grade-date_to.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab20-cert_type IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab20-cert_type = 'M'.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab20 FROM gr_outtab20.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_rtwp_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab20 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab20 INTO gr_outtab20.
    w_next_rtwp_seq_no = gr_outtab20-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_rtwp_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab20 BY date_from DESCENDING
                      plan_no   DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_RTWP

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MR
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mr.

  DATA : l_category         TYPE zim_in_cert-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cert-status,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_user_group       TYPE zim_access_category.

  REFRESH gt_outtab27.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_medrpt
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab27
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab27 BY med_prac.
  CLEAR : zim_med.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab27 INTO gr_outtab27.
    IF gr_outtab27-med_prac IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab27-name = gr_outtab27-med_other.
    ELSE.
      IF gr_outtab27-med_prac NE zim_med-med_prac.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_med
               WHERE med_prac = gr_outtab27-med_prac.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR zim_med.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab27-name = zim_med-name.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP' AND
       w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab27-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab27-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_4001_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
*        gr_outtab27-upd_icon = icon_change.
        gr_outtab27-upd_icon = icon_display.
      ELSE.
        gr_outtab27-upd_icon = icon_display.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.


    CONCATENATE 'MR' gr_outtab27-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab27-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab27-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab27-doc_icon = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab27-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab27-user_group IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab27-user_group = 'I'.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab27-user_group NE g_user_group AND
       zim_in_mast-hod IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab27-doc_icon IS NOT INITIAL.
        gr_outtab27-doc_icon = icon_cancel.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_outtab27-upd_icon IS NOT INITIAL.
        gr_outtab27-upd_icon = icon_cancel.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab27 FROM gr_outtab27.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab27 BY user_group.
  CLEAR : l_user_group, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab27 INTO gr_outtab27.
    IF gr_outtab27-user_group NE l_user_group.
      l_user_group = gr_outtab27-user_group.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_ACCESS_CATEGORY'
                                     l_user_group
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab27-user_group_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab27 FROM gr_outtab27.
  ENDLOOP.



  CLEAR w_next_mr_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab27 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab27 INTO gr_outtab27.
    w_next_mr_seq_no = gr_outtab27-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_mr_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab27 BY med_rpt_date DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_MR

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_WELFARE_CONTACT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_welfare_contact.

  DATA : tb_contact         TYPE zwel_contact_odata_t,
         l_contact          TYPE zwel_contact_odata,
         tb_pernr           TYPE /iwbep/t_cod_select_options,
         l_pernr            TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.

  REFRESH gt_outtab53.

  l_pernr-sign   = 'I'.
  l_pernr-option = 'EQ'.
  l_pernr-low    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  APPEND l_pernr TO tb_pernr.

  CALL METHOD zcl_wel_class=>retrieve_contact_list
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr         = tb_pernr
    IMPORTING
      tb_contact_list = tb_contact.

  LOOP AT tb_contact INTO l_contact
       WHERE contact_reason = 'W'
          OR contact_reason = 'Y'.
    CLEAR gr_outtab53.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_contact TO gr_outtab53.
    gr_outtab53-disp_icon = icon_display.

    CONCATENATE l_contact-contact_rank
                l_contact-contact_ename
                INTO gr_outtab53-contacted_by_name
                SEPARATED BY ' '.
    SHIFT gr_outtab53-contacted_by_name LEFT DELETING LEADING ' '.
    APPEND gr_outtab53 TO gt_outtab53.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_WELFARE_CONTACT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPENDENTS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_dependents.

  DATA: l_date             TYPE d,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab29.
  SELECT * FROM zim_depend
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab29
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr.

  SORT gt_outtab29 BY stat_from_date.
  CLEAR l_date.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab29 INTO gr_outtab29.
    IF gr_outtab29-stat_from_date = l_date.
      DELETE gt_outtab29.
    ENDIF.
    l_date = gr_outtab29-stat_from_date.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab29 INTO gr_outtab29.
    IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab29-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab29-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab29-del_icon   = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab29-copy_icon  = icon_create_copy.
    gr_outtab29-imp_icon   = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'DEP' gr_outtab29-stat_from_date
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab29-pernr
             AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab29-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      gr_outtab29-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab29-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab29 FROM gr_outtab29.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab29 BY stat_from_date DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPENDENTS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CLAIM_STATUS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_claim_status.

  DATA : l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_reason_desc(60) TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_stat(1)         TYPE c,
         l_reason_closure(1) TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab30.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_claim
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab30
           WHERE pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident
             AND claim_type = w_claim_type.

  SORT gt_outtab30 BY duty_det.

  CLEAR : l_duty, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
    IF gr_outtab30-duty_det NE l_duty.
      l_duty = gr_outtab30-duty_det.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CLAIM_DUTY_DET'
                                     l_duty
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab30-duty_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab30-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab30-imp_icon  = icon_import.
    gr_outtab30-ltr_icon  = icon_word_processing.

*<<< BRD 05 AZORBA - Display status history
    SELECT SINGLE user_processed FROM zim_in_claim_log
      INTO gr_outtab30-user_processed
       WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab30-pernr
         AND incident   = gr_outtab30-incident
         AND claim_type = gr_outtab30-claim_type
         AND begda      = gr_outtab30-begda
         AND endda      = gr_outtab30-endda
         AND date_updated = gr_outtab30-date_updated.
*>>> BRD 05 AZORBA -

    IF w_claim_type = 'H' AND
      ( gr_outtab30-stat = '2' OR
        gr_outtab30-stat = '4' ).
      gr_outtab30-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE 'CS' gr_outtab30-claim_type gr_outtab30-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab30-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab30-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab30-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab30-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      IF w_claim_type = 'W' AND
        ( gr_outtab30-stat = '4' OR
          gr_outtab30-stat = '5' OR
          gr_outtab30-stat = '7' ).
        gr_outtab30-email_icon = icon_mail.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab30 FROM gr_outtab30.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab30 BY stat.
  IF w_claim_type NE 'I'.
    CLEAR : t990a.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
      IF gr_outtab30-stat NE t990a-stat.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM t990a
               WHERE langu = 'E'
                 AND stat  = gr_outtab30-stat.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR t990a.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab30-status_desc = t990a-stext.
      MODIFY gt_outtab30 FROM gr_outtab30.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR : l_stat, l_desc.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
      IF gr_outtab30-stat NE l_stat.
        l_stat = gr_outtab30-stat.
        PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IP_CLAIM_STATUS'
                                       l_stat
                              CHANGING l_desc.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab30-status_desc = l_desc.

      MODIFY gt_outtab30 FROM gr_outtab30.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_next_cs_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab30 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
    w_next_cs_seq_no = gr_outtab30-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_cs_seq_no.


  SORT gt_outtab30 BY begda DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CLAIM_STATUS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CLAIM_STATUS_EPSS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_claim_status_epss.

  DATA : l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_reason_desc(60) TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_stat(1)         TYPE c,
         l_reason_closure(1) TYPE c.

*<<< BRD 05 AZORBA - Set claim type as per duty determination in case management tab
  IF w_epss EQ abap_true.
    IF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.
      w_claim_type = 'E'.
    ELSEIF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'F'.
      w_claim_type = 'F'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
*>>> BRD 05 AZORBA - Set claim type as per duty determination in case management tab


  REFRESH gt_outtab30.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_claim
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab30
           WHERE pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident
             AND claim_type = w_claim_type.

  SORT gt_outtab30 BY duty_det.

  CLEAR : l_duty, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
    IF gr_outtab30-duty_det NE l_duty.
      l_duty = gr_outtab30-duty_det.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CLAIM_DUTY_DET'
                                     l_duty
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab30-duty_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab30-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab30-imp_icon  = icon_import.
    gr_outtab30-ltr_icon  = icon_word_processing.

*<<< BRD 05 AZORBA - Display status history
    SELECT SINGLE user_processed FROM zim_in_claim_log
      INTO gr_outtab30-user_processed
       WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab30-pernr
         AND incident   = gr_outtab30-incident
         AND claim_type = gr_outtab30-claim_type
         AND begda      = gr_outtab30-begda
         AND endda      = gr_outtab30-endda
         AND date_updated = gr_outtab30-date_updated.
*>>> BRD 05 AZORBA -

    CONCATENATE 'CS' gr_outtab30-claim_type gr_outtab30-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab30-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab30-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab30-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab30-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab30 FROM gr_outtab30.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab30 BY stat.
  CLEAR : l_stat, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
    IF gr_outtab30-stat NE l_stat.
      l_stat = gr_outtab30-stat.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IP_CLAIM_STATUS'
                                     l_stat
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab30-status_desc = l_desc.

* Including the reason for closure field
    IF gr_outtab30-reason_closure NE l_reason_closure.
      l_reason_closure = gr_outtab30-reason_closure.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_REASON_CLOSURE'
                                     l_reason_closure
                            CHANGING l_reason_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab30-reason_desc = l_reason_desc.

    MODIFY gt_outtab30 FROM gr_outtab30.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_cs_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab30 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
    w_next_cs_seq_no = gr_outtab30-seq_no.
    CLEAR : l_stat, l_desc.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab30 INTO gr_outtab30.
      IF gr_outtab30-stat NE l_stat.
        l_stat = gr_outtab30-stat.
        PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IP_CLAIM_STATUS'
                                       l_stat
                              CHANGING l_desc.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab30-status_desc = l_desc.

      IF gr_outtab30-reason_closure NE l_reason_closure.
        l_reason_closure = gr_outtab30-reason_closure.
        PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_REASON_CLOSURE'
                                       l_reason_closure
                              CHANGING l_reason_desc.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab30-reason_desc = l_reason_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab30 FROM gr_outtab30.
    ENDLOOP.

    MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab30 TO zim_in_claim.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_cs_seq_no.


  SORT gt_outtab30 BY begda DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CLAIM_STATUS_EPSS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM get_hist_claim_assignments.

  DATA : l_rev_type        TYPE zim_in_req_rev-review_type,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_REQ_REVIEW

*       text

FORM get_req_review.

  DATA : l_rev_type        TYPE zim_in_req_rev-review_type,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab56.

           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab56
           WHERE pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab56 BY endda seq_no DESCENDING.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab56 INTO gr_outtab56.
    IF gr_outtab56-review_type NE l_rev_type.
      l_rev_type = gr_outtab56-review_type.

    gr_outtab56-imp_icon  = icon_import.

                INTO l_doc_unique.

           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab56-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab56-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab56-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab56-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab56 FROM gr_outtab56.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_req_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab56 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab56 INTO gr_outtab56.
    w_next_req_seq_no = gr_outtab56-seq_no.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING gr_outtab56 to zim_in_req_rev.
*    w_review_type = l_rev_type.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_req_seq_no.


  SORT gt_outtab56 BY begda DESCENDING seq_no DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_REQ_REVIEW

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_S49_LEAVE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_s49_leave.

  DATA: l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_date_8(8)       TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab35.
  SELECT * FROM pa0416
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab35
           WHERE pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND ( subty    = 'WCDA'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDB'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDE'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDF'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDR'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDS' ).

  LOOP AT gt_outtab35 INTO gr_outtab35.
    PERFORM read_it_text USING gr_outtab35-pernr
                               '0416'
                               gr_outtab35-subty
                               gr_outtab35-begda
                               gr_outtab35-endda
                               gr_outtab35-seqnr.
    gr_outtab35-date_from = w_text_begda.
    gr_outtab35-date_to   = w_text_endda.
    gr_outtab35-incident  = w_text_incident.
    SHIFT gr_outtab35-incident LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.

    PERFORM validate_date USING    gr_outtab35-date_from
                          CHANGING gr_outtab35-lve_begda.
    PERFORM validate_date USING    gr_outtab35-date_to
                          CHANGING gr_outtab35-lve_endda.

    gr_outtab35-imp_icon  = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE gr_outtab35-subty gr_outtab35-lve_begda
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab35-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab35-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab35-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab35-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab35-numbr_2 = gr_outtab35-numbr.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0007
           WHERE pernr  = gr_outtab35-pernr
             AND begda LE gr_outtab35-lve_begda
             AND endda LE gr_outtab35-lve_endda.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0007.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab35-subty = 'WCDA' OR
       gr_outtab35-subty = 'WCDB'.
      gr_outtab35-lve_days = gr_outtab35-numbr.
      gr_outtab35-lve_hrs  = gr_outtab35-numbr * pa0007-arbst.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab35-lve_hrs  = gr_outtab35-numbr.
      IF pa0007-arbst IS NOT INITIAL.
        gr_outtab35-lve_days = gr_outtab35-numbr / pa0007-arbst.
      ELSE.
        gr_outtab35-lve_days = 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab35 FROM gr_outtab35.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab35 BY qctyp.
  CLEAR : t556v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab35 INTO gr_outtab35.
    IF gr_outtab35-qctyp NE t556v-qctyp.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t556v
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND qctyp = gr_outtab35-qctyp.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t556v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab35-qctyp_desc = t556v-qctxt.

    MODIFY gt_outtab35 FROM gr_outtab35.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab35 BY lve_begda DESCENDING.

  IF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDA'.
    w_s49_numbr_desc = 'Std Days During Period'.
    w_wc_lve_desc    = 'Lve Days During Period'.
  ELSE.
    w_s49_numbr_desc = 'Std Hours During Period'.
    w_wc_lve_desc    = 'Lve Hours During Period'.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : zim_screen-s49_begda, zim_screen-s49_endda,
          zim_screen-s49_numbr, zim_screen-s49_quota,
          zim_screen-s49_std_dur, zim_screen-s49_wc_lve,
          zim_screen-s49_proc, zim_screen-ip_start_date,
          zim_screen-ip_end_date.

  IF w_s49_qctyp IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_NSWPF_LEAVE_BALANCE'
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
        i_date       = sy-datum
      TABLES
        tb_quota_sim = tbl_quota_sim
        tb_values    = cum_values.

    IF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDR'.
      READ TABLE cum_values WITH KEY lvind = 'RL  '.
    ELSEIF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDE'.
      READ TABLE cum_values WITH KEY ktart = '10'.
    ELSEIF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDA'.
      READ TABLE cum_values WITH KEY ktart = '55'.
    ENDIF.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      zim_screen-s49_quota = cum_values-entitle
                           - cum_values-deduct
                           - cum_values-ordered
                           - cum_values-locked
                           - cum_values-encashment.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR zim_screen-s49_quota.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_S49_LEAVE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_IP_LEAVE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ip_leave.

  DATA: l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
         l_date_8(8)       TYPE c,
         l_date            TYPE d.

  REFRESH gt_outtab35.
  SELECT * FROM pa0416
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab35
           WHERE pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND ( subty    = 'WCDA'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDB'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDE'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDF'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDR'
              OR   subty    = 'WCDS' ).

  LOOP AT gt_outtab35 INTO gr_outtab35.
    PERFORM read_it_text USING gr_outtab35-pernr
                               '0416'
                               gr_outtab35-subty
                               gr_outtab35-begda
                               gr_outtab35-endda
                               gr_outtab35-seqnr.
    gr_outtab35-date_from = w_text_begda.
    gr_outtab35-date_to   = w_text_endda.
    gr_outtab35-incident  = w_text_incident.
    SHIFT gr_outtab35-incident LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.

    PERFORM validate_date USING    gr_outtab35-date_from
                          CHANGING gr_outtab35-lve_begda.
    PERFORM validate_date USING    gr_outtab35-date_to
                          CHANGING gr_outtab35-lve_endda.

    gr_outtab35-imp_icon  = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE gr_outtab35-subty gr_outtab35-lve_begda
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab35-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab35-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab35-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab35-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab35-numbr_2 = gr_outtab35-numbr.
    IF gr_outtab35-lve_begda IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_date = gr_outtab35-lve_begda.
    ELSE.
      l_date = sy-datum.
    ENDIF.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0007
           WHERE pernr  = gr_outtab35-pernr
             AND begda LE l_date
             AND endda GE l_date.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0007.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab35-subty = 'WCDA' OR
       gr_outtab35-subty = 'WCDB'.
      gr_outtab35-lve_days = gr_outtab35-numbr.
      gr_outtab35-lve_hrs  = gr_outtab35-numbr * pa0007-arbst.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab35-lve_hrs  = gr_outtab35-numbr.
      IF pa0007-arbst IS NOT INITIAL.
        gr_outtab35-lve_days = gr_outtab35-numbr / pa0007-arbst.
      ELSE.
        gr_outtab35-lve_days = 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab35 FROM gr_outtab35.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab35 BY qctyp.
  CLEAR : t556v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab35 INTO gr_outtab35.
    IF gr_outtab35-qctyp NE t556v-qctyp.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t556v
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND qctyp = gr_outtab35-qctyp.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t556v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab35-qctyp_desc = t556v-qctxt.

    MODIFY gt_outtab35 FROM gr_outtab35.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab35 BY lve_begda DESCENDING.

  IF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDB'.
    w_s49_numbr_desc = 'Std Days During Period'.
    w_wc_lve_desc    = 'Lve Days During Period'.
  ELSE.
    w_s49_numbr_desc = 'Std Hours During Period'.
    w_wc_lve_desc    = 'Lve Hours During Period'.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : zim_screen-ipm_pabrj, zim_screen-ipm_pabrp,
          zim_screen-s49_numbr, zim_screen-s49_quota,
          zim_screen-s49_std_dur, zim_screen-s49_wc_lve,
          zim_screen-s49_proc, t549q.

  PERFORM calculate_quota USING sy-datum.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_IP_LEAVE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  calculate_quota
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM calculate_quota USING pv_date  TYPE datum.

  IF w_s49_qctyp IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_NSWPF_LEAVE_BALANCE'
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
        i_date       = pv_date
      TABLES
        tb_quota_sim = tbl_quota_sim
        tb_values    = cum_values.

    IF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDS'.
      READ TABLE cum_values WITH KEY lvind = 'RL  '.
    ELSEIF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDF'.
      READ TABLE cum_values WITH KEY ktart = '10'.
    ELSEIF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDB'.
      READ TABLE cum_values WITH KEY ktart = '55'.
    ENDIF.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      zim_screen-s49_quota = cum_values-entitle
                           - cum_values-deduct
                           - cum_values-ordered
                           - cum_values-locked
                           - cum_values-encashment.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR zim_screen-s49_quota.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "calculate_quota
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_WC_LEAVE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_wc_leave.

  DATA: l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab38.
  SELECT * FROM pa0573
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab38
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
* Start CR 1694 ER 29/11/15
*             AND subty   GE '5500'
*             AND subty   LE '5506'.
           AND subty  BETWEEN '5500' AND '5506' .
  SELECT * FROM pa0573
    APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab38
    WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
    AND subty = '5200'.
* End CR 1694

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0001.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM t001p
         WHERE werks = pa0001-werks
           AND btrtl = pa0001-btrtl.
  IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR t001p.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab38 BY subty.
  CLEAR : t5qpbs2ct.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab38 INTO gr_outtab38.
    IF gr_outtab38-subty NE t5qpbs2ct-lcode.
*  retrieve description
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t5qpbs2ct
             WHERE sprsl = 'E'
               AND moabw = t001p-moabw
               AND lcode = gr_outtab38-subty.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t5qpbs2ct.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab38-lve_desc = t5qpbs2ct-atext.

    MODIFY gt_outtab38 FROM gr_outtab38.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab38 BY begda DESCENDING.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_WC_LEAVE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FIXED_AMOUNT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_fixed_amount.

  DATA: l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab32.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_rec_amt
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab32
           WHERE pernr       = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident    = zim_in_mast-incident
             AND amount_type = w_amount_type.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab32 INTO gr_outtab32.
    gr_outtab32-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab32-imp_icon  = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'FIX' gr_outtab32-amount_type gr_outtab32-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab32-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab32-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab32-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab32-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'FIX' gr_outtab32-amount_type gr_outtab32-seq_no
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab32-desc_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab32-desc_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab32 FROM gr_outtab32.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_fix_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab32 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab32 INTO gr_outtab32.
    w_next_fix_seq_no = gr_outtab32-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_fix_seq_no.


  SORT gt_outtab32 BY begda DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_FIXED_AMOUNT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_COMPARABLE_EARNINGS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_comparable_earnings.

  DATA: l_duty            TYPE zim_in_claim-duty_det,
         l_desc(60)        TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique      TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab37.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_comp
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab37
           WHERE pernr       = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident    = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab37 INTO gr_outtab37.
    gr_outtab37-del_icon  = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab37-imp_icon  = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'COMPEARN' gr_outtab37-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab37-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab37-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab37-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab37-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab37 FROM gr_outtab37.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_comp_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab37 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab37 INTO gr_outtab37.
    w_next_comp_seq_no = gr_outtab37-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_comp_seq_no.


  SORT gt_outtab37 BY begda DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_COMPARABLE_EARNINGS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PIAWE_DETAIL_AND_HISTORY
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_piawe_detail_and_history.

  DATA:
    lt_t512t                  TYPE HASHED TABLE OF t512t
                              WITH UNIQUE KEY lgart,
    lt_t530f                  TYPE HASHED TABLE OF t530f
                              WITH UNIQUE KEY preas,
    ls_t512t                  TYPE t512t,
    ls_t530f                  TYPE t530f,
    lv_doc_unique             TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique,
    lv_incident_like          TYPE c LENGTH 20.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <ls_outtab46>             TYPE gs_outtab_type46.

  CLEAR:
    gt_outtab46[],
    w_piawe_det,
    w_piawe_det_old.

* Store the ids of the current details.
  w_piawe_det-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  w_piawe_det-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

* Build search string for Incident number.
  lv_incident_like = zim_in_mast-incident.
  SHIFT lv_incident_like LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
  CONCATENATE '%' lv_incident_like '%'
              INTO lv_incident_like.

* Get PIAWE Wage Types from P0014.
  SELECT * FROM pa0014
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab46
    WHERE pernr EQ zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND subty IN
             (zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_pre_52,
              zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_post_52,
              zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_new)
      AND zuord LIKE lv_incident_like.

  IF gt_outtab46[] IS INITIAL.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab46
       BY endda DESCENDING
          lgart ASCENDING.

* Get Wage Type descriptions.
  SELECT *
    FROM t512t
    INTO TABLE lt_t512t
    WHERE sprsl EQ sy-langu
      AND molga EQ zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_molga_aust
      AND lgart IN
           (zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_pre_52,
            zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_post_52,
            zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_new).

* Get Reason descriptions.
  SELECT *
    FROM t530f
    INTO TABLE lt_t530f
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_outtab46
    WHERE sprsl EQ sy-langu
      AND infty EQ '0014'
      AND preas EQ gt_outtab46-preas.

* Update the PIAWE history.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab46 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab46>.
*   Set the Manually Entered flag.
    IF <ls_outtab46>-preas EQ
                 zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_preas-manual_piawe OR
       <ls_outtab46>-preas EQ
                 zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_preas-transitional_piawe.
      <ls_outtab46>-manually_entered = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
*   Set the Wage Type description.
    IF ls_t512t-lgart NE <ls_outtab46>-lgart.
      READ TABLE lt_t512t INTO ls_t512t
                 WITH TABLE KEY lgart = <ls_outtab46>-lgart.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR ls_t512t.
        ls_t512t-lgart = <ls_outtab46>-lgart.
        ls_t512t-lgtxt = <ls_outtab46>-lgart.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    <ls_outtab46>-lgtxt = ls_t512t-lgtxt.
*   Set the Reason description.
    IF <ls_outtab46>-preas IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF ls_t530f-preas NE <ls_outtab46>-preas.
        READ TABLE lt_t530f INTO ls_t530f
                   WITH TABLE KEY preas = <ls_outtab46>-preas.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR ls_t530f.
          ls_t530f-preas = <ls_outtab46>-preas.
          ls_t530f-rtext = <ls_outtab46>-preas.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    <ls_outtab46>-rtext = ls_t530f-rtext.
*   Delete Icon.
    <ls_outtab46>-del_icon = icon_delete.
  ENDLOOP.

* PIAWE detail.
  READ TABLE gt_outtab46 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab46>
       WITH KEY lgart =
            zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_pre_52.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    w_piawe_det-piawe_pre_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-betrg.
    w_piawe_det-manual_pre_52_wk = <ls_outtab46>-manually_entered.
    w_piawe_det-begda_pre_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-begda.
    w_piawe_det-endda_pre_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-endda.
  ENDIF.
  READ TABLE gt_outtab46 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab46>
       WITH KEY lgart =
            zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_post_52.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    w_piawe_det-piawe_post_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-betrg.
    w_piawe_det-manual_post_52_wk = <ls_outtab46>-manually_entered.
    w_piawe_det-begda_post_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-begda.
    w_piawe_det-endda_post_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-endda.
  ENDIF.

  READ TABLE gt_outtab46 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab46>
       WITH KEY lgart =
            zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_piawe_new.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    w_piawe_det-piawe_new_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-betrg.
    w_piawe_det-manual_new_52_wk = <ls_outtab46>-manually_entered.
    w_piawe_det-begda_new_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-begda.
    w_piawe_det-endda_new_52_wk  = <ls_outtab46>-endda.
  ENDIF.

  w_piawe_det_old = w_piawe_det.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PIAWE_DETAIL_AND_HISTORY

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  get_concurrent_details
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_concurrent_details.

  DATA:
    lv_valid_outtab47         TYPE abap_bool.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <ls_outtab47>             TYPE gs_outtab_type47.

  CLEAR:
    gt_outtab47[],
    w_prim_det,
    w_prim_det_old.

* Store the ids of the current details.
  w_prim_det-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  w_prim_det-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

* Get Primary Claim details.
  SELECT *
    FROM zim_in_prim
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab47
    WHERE pernr    EQ zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND conc_ext_claim = abap_true.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*   delete Inactive records
    DELETE gt_outtab47 WHERE inactive = 'X'.

* get concurrent claim for this incident
    READ TABLE gt_outtab47 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab47> WITH KEY pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
                                                            incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim = <ls_outtab47>-conc_ext_claim.
      w_prim_det-begda_cec      = <ls_outtab47>-begda.
      w_prim_det-endda_cec      = <ls_outtab47>-endda.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING <ls_outtab47> TO w_cec_claim.
    ENDIF.

    SORT gt_outtab47 DESCENDING
         BY begda endda.
*   get claim number
    LOOP AT gt_outtab47 ASSIGNING <ls_outtab47>.
      PERFORM get_claim_no    USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                    zim_in_mast-incident
                           CHANGING <ls_outtab47>-wclmn.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.


  w_prim_det_old = w_prim_det.

ENDFORM.                    " get_concurrent_details

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ECP_DETAIL_AND_HISTORY
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Get External Claim Percentage details and history.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ecp_detail_and_history.

  DATA:
    lv_doc_unique             TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  CLEAR:
    gt_outtab48[],
    w_ecp_det,
    w_ecp_det_old.

* Store the ids of the current details.
  w_ecp_det-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  w_ecp_det-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

* Get External Claim History from PA0014 SubType 9WUH..
  SELECT *
    FROM pa0014
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab48
    WHERE pernr EQ zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND subty EQ
        zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-unsworn_wc_pcent_for_apport.

  IF gt_outtab48[] IS INITIAL.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab48 DESCENDING
       BY begda endda.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab48 INTO gr_outtab48.
    IF gr_outtab48-begda LE sy-datum AND
       gr_outtab48-endda GE sy-datum.
      w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim = gr_outtab48-anzhl.
      w_ecp_det-begda        = gr_outtab48-begda.
      w_ecp_det-endda        = gr_outtab48-endda.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab48-del_icon = icon_delete.
    MODIFY gt_outtab48 FROM gr_outtab48
                       TRANSPORTING del_icon.
  ENDLOOP.

  w_ecp_det_old = w_ecp_det.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_ECP_DETAIL_AND_HISTORY

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_EPB_DETAIL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Get Entitlement period bucket detail.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_epb_detail.

  CONSTANTS:
    lc_stat_finalised           TYPE zim_in_claim-stat VALUE '8'.

  DATA:
    lt_epb_weeks_state          TYPE zim_epb_weeks_state_tbl.

  DATA:
    ls_epb_weeks_state          TYPE zim_epb_weeks_state.

  CLEAR w_epb_det.

* Store the ids of the current details.
  w_ecp_det-pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
  w_ecp_det-incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

* Calculate the Entitlement Period Buckets and their capacity.
  CALL FUNCTION 'ZHWC_GET_UNSW_BUCKET_STATE'
    EXPORTING
      i_pernr            = zim_in_mast-pernr
      i_incident         = zim_in_mast-incident
      i_dateincident     = zim_incident-dateincident
      i_wc_close_date    = zim_in_cm-wc_close_date
    IMPORTING
      et_epb_weeks_state = lt_epb_weeks_state.

* Find the first bucket with capacity.
  LOOP AT lt_epb_weeks_state INTO ls_epb_weeks_state
          WHERE bucket_enclosed EQ space AND
                bucket_full     EQ space.
    w_epb_det-bucket = ls_epb_weeks_state-bucket.
    w_epb_det-text   = ls_epb_weeks_state-bucket_desc.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_EPB_DETAIL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NEW_DEPENDENTS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_new_dependents.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cert-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cert-status,
         l_mc_ben           TYPE zim_mc_ben_status,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_days             TYPE i,
         l_days2            TYPE i,
         l_pernr            TYPE pa0002-pernr,
         l_orgeh            TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc       TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         l_incident         TYPE zim_in_cert-incident,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab34.

  IF w_gao IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab34
           WHERE gao = w_gao
             AND hod = ' '
             AND ( inc_type = 'I'
              OR   inc_type = 'R' ).
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab34
           WHERE hod = ' '
             AND ( inc_type = 'I'
              OR   inc_type = 'R' ).
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab34 BY pernr incident.
  CLEAR : l_pernr, l_incident.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab34 INTO gr_outtab34.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab34-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab34-incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_in_cm.
      zcl_hr_utilities=>is_person_sworn_unsworn(
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr   = gr_outtab34-pernr
        IMPORTING
          e_unsworn = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim ).
    ENDIF.
* Ignore closed or pending cases
    IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C' OR
       zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'P'.
      DELETE gt_outtab34.
    ELSEIF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim EQ abap_true.
*     Ignore unsworn claims.
      DELETE gt_outtab34.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab34-depend_sent = zim_in_cm-depend_sent.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab34-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab34-incident.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_incident.
      ENDIF.
      CLEAR l_days.
      IF zim_incident-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
               WHERE pernr = gr_outtab34-pernr
                 AND casno = gr_outtab34-incident.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          l_days = sy-datum - pa9082-idate.
          gr_outtab34-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        l_days = sy-datum - zim_incident-dateincident.
        gr_outtab34-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      ENDIF.
* Ignore if case less than 20 weeks
      IF l_days LT 140.
        DELETE gt_outtab34.
      ELSE.
        MODIFY gt_outtab34 FROM gr_outtab34.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab34 BY pernr
                      incident DESCENDING.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_outtab34 COMPARING pernr.

  IF zim_screen-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_dependents_org.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab34 INTO gr_outtab34.
    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab34-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab34-orgeh.
    IF zim_screen-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab34-orgeh NOT IN r_orgeh.
        DELETE gt_outtab34.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab34 FROM gr_outtab34.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab34 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab34 INTO gr_outtab34.
    IF gr_outtab34-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab34-orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab34-orgeh_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab34 FROM gr_outtab34.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR pa0002.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab34 INTO gr_outtab34.
    IF gr_outtab34-pernr NE pa0002-pernr.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab34-pernr
               AND begda   LE sy-datum
               AND endda   GE sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR pa0002.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab34-nachn      = pa0002-nachn.
    gr_outtab34-vorna      = pa0002-vorna.
    gr_outtab34-email_icon = icon_mail.
    MODIFY gt_outtab34 FROM gr_outtab34.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab34 INTO gr_outtab34.
    SELECT * FROM zim_depend
           INTO TABLE tbl_zim_depend
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab34-pernr.
    CLEAR tbl_zim_depend.
    SORT tbl_zim_depend BY stat_from_date DESCENDING
                           dep_to_date.
    LOOP AT tbl_zim_depend.
      EXIT.
    ENDLOOP.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING tbl_zim_depend TO gr_outtab34.
* If the Stat Dec is due to expire in less than 20 days or
* a person within the stat dec is due to expire - process
    IF gr_outtab34-stat_to_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_days  = gr_outtab34-stat_to_date - sy-datum.
      IF gr_outtab34-dep_to_date IS INITIAL.
        l_days2 = 99.
      ELSE.
        l_days2 = gr_outtab34-dep_to_date - sy-datum.
      ENDIF.
      IF l_days  GT 20 AND
         l_days2 GT 20.
        DELETE gt_outtab34.
      ELSE.
        CONCATENATE 'DEP' gr_outtab34-stat_from_date
                    INTO l_doc_unique.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
               WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab34-pernr
                 AND incident   = gr_outtab34-incident
                 AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          gr_outtab34-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
          gr_outtab34-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
        ENDIF.
        gr_outtab34-upd_icon     = icon_change.
        MODIFY gt_outtab34 FROM gr_outtab34.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab34 BY dateincident.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_NEW_DEPENDENTS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_IMEP
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_imep .

  DATA: l_imep_type        TYPE zim_in_imep-imep_type,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab22.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_imep
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab22
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab22 BY imep_type.
  CLEAR : l_imep_type, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab22 INTO gr_outtab22.
    IF gr_outtab22-imep_type NE l_imep_type.
      l_imep_type = gr_outtab22-imep_type.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IMEP_TYPE'
                                     l_imep_type
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab22-imep_desc = l_desc.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IMEP_APP_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab22-app_sima
                          CHANGING gr_outtab22-sima_stat.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IMEP_APP_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab22-app_mgmt
                          CHANGING gr_outtab22-mgmt_stat.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IMEP_REC_WORK_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab22-rec_work_status
                          CHANGING gr_outtab22-rec_work_desc.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_prov
           WHERE prov_code = gr_outtab22-prov_code.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab22-company            = zim_prov-company.
      gr_outtab22-consultant         = zim_prov-consultant.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP' AND
       w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab22-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab22-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab22 FROM gr_outtab22.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_imep_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab22 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab22 INTO gr_outtab22.
    w_next_imep_seq_no = gr_outtab22-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_imep_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab22 BY seq_no DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_IMEP

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FTCAX
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ftcax.

  DATA: l_imep_type        TYPE zim_in_imep-imep_type,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab28.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_ftcax
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab28
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab28 INTO gr_outtab28.
    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTCAX_APP_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab28-sima_ref_status
                          CHANGING gr_outtab28-sima_ref_stat.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTCAX_REC_WORK_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab28-rec_work_status
                          CHANGING gr_outtab28-rec_work_desc.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTCAX_SIMA_RESULT_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab28-sima_res_status
                          CHANGING gr_outtab28-sima_res_stat.


    IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab28-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab28-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab28 FROM gr_outtab28.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_ftcax_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab28 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab28 INTO gr_outtab28.
    w_next_ftcax_seq_no = gr_outtab28-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_ftcax_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab28 BY seq_no DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_FTCAX

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FTC
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ftc .

  DATA: l_desc(60)         TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab23.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_ftc
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab23
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  CLEAR : l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab23 INTO gr_outtab23.
    gr_outtab23-ftco_date = zim_in_mast-ftco_date.
    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTC_APP_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab23-app_tl
                          CHANGING gr_outtab23-tl_stat.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTC_APP_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab23-app_mgmt
                          CHANGING gr_outtab23-mgmt_stat.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTC_APP_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab23-app_com
                          CHANGING gr_outtab23-com_stat.

    PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_FTC_CASE_STATUS'
                                   gr_outtab23-case_status
                          CHANGING gr_outtab23-case_stat.

    IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab23-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab23-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab23 FROM gr_outtab23.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_FTC

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MD
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_md .

  DATA: l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_md_status        TYPE zim_screen-md_status.

  REFRESH gt_outtab24.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_md
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab24
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.


  LOOP AT gt_outtab24 INTO gr_outtab24.
    IF gr_outtab24-sass_ltr_sent_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '16'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-sass_ltr_rcvd_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '15'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-sass_sub_gen_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '14'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-sass_ltr_gen_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '13'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-app_ltr_sent_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '12'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-app_ltr_rcvd_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '11'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-app_sub_gen_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '10'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-app_ltr_gen_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '09'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-cop_ltr_sent_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '08'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-cop_ltr_rcvd_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '07'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-cop_sub_gen_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '06'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-cop_ltr_gen_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '05'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-mdrp_recom IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '04'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-mdrp_sub_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '03'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-info_pack_sent IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '02'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-admin_fin_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '20'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-admin_recom IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '19'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-admin_sub_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '18'.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab24-admin_ltr_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab24-md_status = '17'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_ftc
             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab24-mdu_date = zim_in_ftc-mdu_date.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab24-disp_icon = icon_display.
    MODIFY gt_outtab24 FROM gr_outtab24.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab24 BY md_status.
  CLEAR : l_md_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab24 INTO gr_outtab24.
    IF gr_outtab24-md_status NE l_md_status.
      l_md_status = gr_outtab24-md_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MD_STATUS'
                                     l_md_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab24-status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab24 FROM gr_outtab24.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_MD

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PRE88_LEG
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_pre88_leg .


  REFRESH gt_outtab50.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_pre_leg
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab50
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab50 INTO gr_outtab50.

    IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab50-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab50-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab50-legal_ltr_comp IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab50-rev_icon   = icon_check.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab50-rev_icon   = icon_okay.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab50-legal_ltr_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab50-tl_approved    IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab50-tl_icon   = icon_allow.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab50-tl_approved  IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab50-tl_icon   = icon_okay.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab50-tl_approved IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab50-mgr_approved    IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab50-app_icon   = icon_allow.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab50-mgr_approved  IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab50-app_icon   = icon_okay.
      gr_outtab50-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab50-legal_ltr_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
       ( gr_outtab50-tl_approved  IS INITIAL OR
         gr_outtab50-mgr_approved IS INITIAL ).
      gr_outtab50-ret_icon   = icon_reject.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'PRE88LEGRET' gr_outtab50-seq_no
           INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab50-notes_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab50-notes_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.


    MODIFY gt_outtab50 FROM gr_outtab50.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_prel_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab50 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab50 INTO gr_outtab50.
    w_next_prel_seq_no = gr_outtab50-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_prel_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab50 BY seq_no DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PRE88_LEG

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PRE88_INV
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_pre88_inv .

  DATA : tb_pa9093          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF pa9093,
         l_pa9093           TYPE pa9093,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab51.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_pre_inv
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab51
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab51 INTO gr_outtab51.

    IF w_3600_action = 'DISP' OR
       gr_outtab51-proc_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab51-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab51-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab51-inv_comp IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab51-rev_icon   = icon_check.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab51-rev_icon   = icon_okay.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab51-inv_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab51-inv_app  IS INITIAL     AND
       gr_outtab51-amtpd GE 1000.
      gr_outtab51-mgr_icon   = icon_allow.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab51-inv_app  IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab51-amtpd GE 1000.
      gr_outtab51-mgr_icon   = icon_okay.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab51-inv_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab51-inv_app  IS INITIAL     AND
       gr_outtab51-amtpd LT 1000.
      gr_outtab51-app_icon   = icon_allow.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab51-inv_app  IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab51-amtpd LT 1000.
      gr_outtab51-app_icon   = icon_okay.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab51-inv_app   IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab51-proc_date IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab51-proc_icon  = icon_workflow_process.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab51-inv_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gr_outtab51-inv_app  IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab51-ret_icon   = icon_reject.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab51-source     = 'New'.

    IF gr_outtab51-tl_approved_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab51-approved_date = gr_outtab51-tl_approved_date.
    ELSEIF gr_outtab51-mgr_approved_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab51-approved_date = gr_outtab51-mgr_approved_date.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'PRE88INVRET' gr_outtab51-seq_no
           INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab51-notes_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab51-notes_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE 'PRE88INV' gr_outtab51-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab51-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab51-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab51-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab51-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab51 FROM gr_outtab51.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR w_next_prei_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab51 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab51 INTO gr_outtab51.
    w_next_prei_seq_no = gr_outtab51-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_prei_seq_no.

  SELECT * FROM pa9093 INTO TABLE tb_pa9093
         WHERE pernr = pa9076-pernr
           AND objps = pa9076-objps
           AND casno = pa9076-casno.

  LOOP AT tb_pa9093 INTO l_pa9093.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab51 INTO gr_outtab51
         WHERE refno = l_pa9093-refno
           AND recdt = l_pa9093-recdt
           AND zzvendor = l_pa9093-zzvendor.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR gr_outtab51.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING l_pa9093 TO gr_outtab51.
      gr_outtab51-upd_icon      = icon_display.
      gr_outtab51-source        = 'Old'.
      gr_outtab51-created_date  = l_pa9093-aedtm..
      APPEND gr_outtab51 TO gt_outtab51.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab51 BY seq_no DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PRE88_INV

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PRE88_SEC_CLAIMS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_pre88_sec_claims .

  DATA : tb_pa9093          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF pa9093,
         l_pa9093           TYPE pa9093,
         l_inc_status(1)    TYPE c,
         l_locationt        TYPE zp902_locationt,
         l_inc_inj          TYPE zim_inc_inj,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab52.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_pre88
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab52
           WHERE pernr            = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND parent_incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab52 INTO gr_outtab52.

    IF w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab52-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab52-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab52 FROM gr_outtab52.

    CLEAR zim_incident.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab52-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab52-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab52-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_inj
           INTO l_inc_inj
           WHERE pernr       = gr_outtab52-pernr
             AND identnr     = gr_outtab52-incident
             AND main_injury = 'X'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab52-main_loc = l_inc_inj-location_code.
    ELSE.
      CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
                  INTO w_fieldname.
      LOOP AT nametab
           WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
        gr_outtab52-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab52-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab52-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab52-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab52-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
        gr_outtab52-stats  = pa9076-stats.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab52 FROM gr_outtab52.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab52 BY stats.
  CLEAR : t990a.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab52 INTO gr_outtab52.
    IF gr_outtab52-stats NE t990a-stat.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t990a
             WHERE langu = 'E'
               AND stat  = gr_outtab52-stats.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t990a.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab52-stats_desc = t990a-stext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab52 FROM gr_outtab52.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab52 BY current_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab52 INTO gr_outtab52.
    IF gr_outtab52-current_status NE l_inc_status.
      l_inc_status = gr_outtab52-current_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_CURRENT_STATUS'
                                     l_inc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab52-current_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab52 FROM gr_outtab52.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab52 BY ass_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab52 INTO gr_outtab52.
    IF gr_outtab52-ass_status NE l_inc_status.
      l_inc_status = gr_outtab52-ass_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_ASS_STATUS'
                                     l_inc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab52-ass_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab52 FROM gr_outtab52.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab52 BY main_loc.
  CLEAR : l_locationt.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab52 INTO gr_outtab52.
    IF gr_outtab52-main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab52-main_loc NE l_locationt-location_code.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zp902_locationt
               INTO l_locationt
               WHERE  sprsl = 'E'
                 AND  location_code = gr_outtab52-main_loc.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_locationt.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab52-main_injury  = l_locationt-location_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab52 FROM gr_outtab52.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.



  SORT gt_outtab52 BY incident DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PRE88_SEC_CLAIMS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_WC_RECOUP
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_wc_recoup .

  SELECT * FROM zim_recoup_doc
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab21
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SELECT * FROM zim_recoup
         APPENDING CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab21
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND recoup_processed = ' '.


  SORT gt_outtab21 BY pernr incident per_begda begda date_processed
                     run_no seq_no.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_WC_RECOUP
