*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_REM
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_rem.

  DATA: l_created_by           TYPE zim_in_rem-created_by,
         l_ass_to               TYPE zim_in_rem-ass_to,
         l_note_cat             TYPE zim_in_rem-note_cat,
         l_order                TYPE i,
         l_pernr                TYPE pa0001-pernr,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.

  REFRESH gt_outtab17.
  IF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_screen-ass_to IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_rem
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab17
             WHERE ( ass_to  = zim_screen-ass_to
                OR   ass_emp = w_ima )
               AND note_comp = ' '.
  ELSEIF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_rem
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab17
             WHERE note_comp = ' '
               AND ass_emp   = w_ima.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_rem
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab17
             WHERE ass_to  = zim_screen-ass_to
               AND note_comp = ' '.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY pernr incident.
  CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF gr_outtab17-pernr    NE zim_in_mast-pernr OR
       gr_outtab17-incident NE zim_in_mast-incident.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab17-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab17-incident.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_in_mast.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-ima = zim_in_mast-ima.
    gr_outtab17-ftc = zim_in_mast-ftc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR zim_in_cm.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF gr_outtab17-pernr    NE zim_in_cm-pernr OR
       gr_outtab17-incident NE zim_in_cm-incident.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab17-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab17-incident.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_in_cm.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-wc_advisor = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor.
    gr_outtab17-ip_advisor = zim_in_cm-ip_advisor.
    gr_outtab17-si_advisor = zim_in_cm-si_advisor.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF zim_screen-ass_to = 'S' OR
       zim_screen-ass_to = 'I' OR
       zim_screen-ass_to = 'F'.
      IF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF gr_outtab17-ass_emp IS NOT INITIAL AND
           gr_outtab17-ass_emp NE w_ima.
          DELETE gt_outtab17.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        IF gr_outtab17-ass_emp IS INITIAL AND
           gr_outtab17-ima NE w_ima AND
           gr_outtab17-ftc NE w_ima.
          DELETE gt_outtab17.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab17-review_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab17-review_date GT sy-datum AND
         w_all_remind IS INITIAL.
        DELETE gt_outtab17.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF zim_screen-ass_to = 'W'.
      IF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF gr_outtab17-ass_emp IS NOT INITIAL AND
           gr_outtab17-ass_emp NE w_ima.
          DELETE gt_outtab17.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        IF gr_outtab17-ass_emp IS INITIAL AND
           gr_outtab17-wc_advisor NE w_ima AND
           gr_outtab17-ip_advisor NE w_ima AND
           gr_outtab17-si_advisor NE w_ima.
          DELETE gt_outtab17.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab17-review_date IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab17-review_date GT sy-datum AND
         w_all_remind IS INITIAL.
        DELETE gt_outtab17.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY pernr incident.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab17-pernr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab17-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      gr_outtab17-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
    ENDIF.
    IF gr_outtab17-review_date IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab17-sort_date = gr_outtab17-created_date.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab17-sort_date = gr_outtab17-review_date.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY ass_to.
  CLEAR : l_ass_to, w_domvalue_l, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF gr_outtab17-ass_to NE l_ass_to.
      l_ass_to = gr_outtab17-ass_to.
      w_domvalue_l = l_ass_to.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_IM_ROLE'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-ass_to_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY note_cat.
  CLEAR : l_note_cat, w_domvalue_l, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF gr_outtab17-note_cat NE l_note_cat.
      l_note_cat = gr_outtab17-note_cat.
      w_domvalue_l = l_note_cat.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_NOTE_CATEGORY'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-cat_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab17-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab17-orgeh.
*   Org Structire
    IF gr_outtab17-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab17-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab17-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = gr_outtab17-orgeh.
      ENDIF.
*   Object text
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    zhorgstruc-lvl4_code
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.

    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab17-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab17-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab17-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17."MD

  ENDLOOP.
* Display the oldest at the top but the reminders are grouped
* by employee

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY sort_date.
  l_order = 1.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    gr_outtab17-sort_order = l_order.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
    ADD 1 TO l_order.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY pernr sort_order.
  CLEAR l_pernr.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    IF gr_outtab17-pernr NE l_pernr.
      l_order = gr_outtab17-sort_order.
      l_pernr = gr_outtab17-pernr.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab17-sort_order = l_order.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.

* Assigned to Name new field.MD
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
               WHERE pernr = gr_outtab17-ass_emp
               AND begda LE sy-datum
               AND endda GE sy-datum.
    gr_outtab17-ass_emp_name = pa0001-ename.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.
* end of Changes.MD

* Start of CR3197 DEVK9HRFUN
  LOOP AT gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
               WHERE pernr = gr_outtab17-ima
               AND begda LE sy-datum
               AND endda GE sy-datum.
    gr_outtab17-ima_name = pa0001-ename.
    MODIFY gt_outtab17 FROM gr_outtab17.
  ENDLOOP.
* End of CR3197 DEVK9HRFUN

  SORT gt_outtab17 BY sort_order
                      sort_date.

  gt_outtab17_old[] = gt_outtab17[].

ENDFORM.                                                  " GET_REM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  set_defaults_1000
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_defaults_1000.
  PERFORM set_defaults_1001.
  PERFORM set_defaults_1002.
ENDFORM.                    "set_defaults_1000
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  set_defaults_1001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_defaults_1001.
  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

* default time when NWR
  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     p9001-timeincident IS INITIAL.
    p9001-timeincident = '010000'.
  ENDIF.

* default Activity when NWR
  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     p9001-activity_new IS INITIAL.
    p9001-activity_new = '039'.
  ENDIF.

* default Cause when NWR
  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND p9001-cause_new  IS INITIAL.
    p9001-cause_new = '031'.
    p9001-other_cause = 'Non-work related'.
  ENDIF.

* default place where the activity happened in case of NWR
  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND p9001-where_new  IS INITIAL.
    p9001-where_new  = '006'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND p9001-datereported IS INITIAL.
    p9001-datereported = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND p9001-time_reported IS INITIAL.
    p9001-time_reported = sy-uzeit.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND p9001-personreportedo IS INITIAL.
    p9001-personreportedo = 'Other'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND  p9001-personreptotel IS INITIAL.
    p9001-personreptotel = 'Other'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "set_defaults_1001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  set_defaults_1002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_defaults_1002.

  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab1 LINES w_lines.
  IF w_lines = 0 AND
     p9001-nearmiss = '3'.
    CLEAR gr_outtab1.
    gr_outtab1-location_code  = '035'.
    gr_outtab1-location_desc  = 'Other/Unspecified'.
    gr_outtab1-other_location = 'Non-Work Related'.
    gr_outtab1-nature_code    = '022'.
    gr_outtab1-nature_desc    = 'Other/Unspecified'.
    gr_outtab1-other_nature   = 'Non-Work Related'.
    gr_outtab1-main_injury    = 'X'.
    APPEND gr_outtab1 TO gt_outtab1.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    "set_defaults_1002
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_1000
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_1000 .

  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-pernr IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employee that had the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = p9001-pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Employee Number'.
  ENDIF.

  IF pa0001-persg = 'G'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Incidents can only be entered'
                      'for NSWPF Employees'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Type of'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group EQ 'S'.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
           WHERE ( subty = '9PSS' OR subty =   '9PSP'
               OR  subty = '9PSO' OR subty =   '9PSR'
               OR  subty = 'ZPSS' OR subty =   'ZPSR' )
             AND pernr = p9001-pernr.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Incidents can only be processed'
                        'for Pre 88 Police Officers'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3'.
    w_inc_where = '006'.
    w_inc_act   = '039'.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_no_pernr.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_1000

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_1001
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_1001 .

  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-dateincident IS INITIAL OR
     p9001-timeincident IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date and Time of the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-dateincident GT sy-datum OR
   ( p9001-dateincident EQ sy-datum AND
     p9001-timeincident GT sy-uzeit ).
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date and Time of the'
                      'Incident cannot be in the'
                      'future'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-activity_new IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Activity at the Time of the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     w_inc_act NE '039'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please select Non Work Related for'
                      'Activity at time on Incident'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-cause_new IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Cause of the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-where_new IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Where the Incident happened'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     w_inc_where NE '006'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please select Non Work Related for'
                      'Where did the Incident happen?'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-datereported IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date the Incident was'
                      'reported to the Employer'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-personreportedo IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Person the Incident was'
                      'reported to'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-personreptotel IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Telephone Number of the'
                      'Person the Incident was'
                      'reported to'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF gen_txt_text IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table                  = gt_outtxt1
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp               = 1
        error_cntl_call_method = 2
        error_dp_create        = 3
        potential_data_loss    = 4.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_1001

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  validate_incident_desc
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_incident_desc.
  IF gen_txt_text IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table                  = gt_outtxt1
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp               = 1
        error_cntl_call_method = 2
        error_dp_create        = 3
        potential_data_loss    = 4.
  ENDIF.

* Incident description is mandatory in case of NWR Incident
  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     gt_outtxt1 IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Incident description must be entered'
                      'for Non Work Related Incidents'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "validate_incident_desc
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_1003
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_1003 .

  IF p9001-treatmentopt IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Treatment that was'
                      'received must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-treatmentopt NE '1'.
    IF p9001-fitnessstat IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The employees Current'
                        'Fitness must be entered'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_1003


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_1005
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_1005 .

  IF w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-dateincident IS INITIAL OR
     p9001-timeincident IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date and Time of the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-dateincident GT sy-datum OR
   ( p9001-dateincident EQ sy-datum AND
     p9001-timeincident GT sy-uzeit ).
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date and Time of the'
                      'Incident cannot be in the'
                      'future'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_inc_p902-where_did_the_incident_happen IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Activity at the Time of the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     w_inc_act NE '039'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please select Non Work Related for'
                      'Activity at time on Incident'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_inc_p902-hazardsubclass IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Cause of the'
                      'Incident must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-nearmiss = '3' AND
     w_inc_where NE '006'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please select Non Work Related for'
                      'Where did the Incident happen?'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-datereported IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date the Incident was'
                      'reported to the Employer'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF gen_txt_text IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD gen_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
      IMPORTING
        table                  = gt_outtxt1
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp               = 1
        error_cntl_call_method = 2
        error_dp_create        = 3
        potential_data_loss    = 4.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_1005

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_1100
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_1100 .

  IF w_pernr IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter an Employee Number'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = w_pernr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Employee Number'.
  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group EQ 'S' OR
     g_user_group EQ 'M'.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
           WHERE ( subty = '9PSS' OR subty =   '9PSP'
               OR  subty = '9PSO' OR subty =   '9PSR'
               OR  subty = 'ZPSS' OR subty =   'ZPSR' )
             AND pernr = w_pernr.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
* Check for terminated before 2000.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
             WHERE pernr = w_pernr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Incidents can only be processed'
                          'for Pre 88 Police Officers'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  w_emp_name = pa0001-ename.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_1100


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_1200
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_1200 .

  IF w_old_identnr IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The old Incident Number'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = w_pernr
           AND incident = w_old_identnr.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Incident Number'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_old_identnr = p9001-identnr.
    CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The old Incident Number cannot'
                      'be the new Incident Number'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_1200


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_2101
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_2101 .

  IF zim_in_mast-ima IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter an Injury Management Advisor'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
         WHERE ima = zim_in_mast-ima.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Injury Management Advisor'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-ima
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Injury Management Advisor'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-info_consent IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-info_date IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Date the Info Consent'
                      'Form was received'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-info_consent IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-info_date IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Select the Info Consent Form Received'
                      'Checkbox if the Form has been received'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-last_day_duty IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_mast-last_day_duty GT sy-datum.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Last Day of Duty cannot be'
                        'in the future'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-inc_status = 'C' AND
     zim_in_mast-closure_status IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Closure Status must be set'
                      'when the Injury Management Status'
                      'is closed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-mmi_received IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-mmi_received_dt IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Date the MMI was received'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-mmi_received IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast-mmi_received_dt.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-change_of_goal IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-change_of_goal_date IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Date the Goal was'
                      'Changed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-refer_trans IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-refer_trans_date IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Date the case was'
                      'referred to Transition'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-refer_rehab IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-refer_rehab_date IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Date the case was'
                      'referred to a Rehab Provider'.
  ENDIF.


  w_ima_name = pa0001-ename.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_2101

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_2201
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_2201 .

  IF zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'Invalid Claims Officer/Assistant Claims Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'Invalid Claims Officer/Assistant Claims Officer'.
    ENDIF.
    w_ima_name = pa0001-ename.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_ima_name.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-ass_status   = 'T'.
    CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'The Claims Officer/Assistant Claims Officer'
        'must be entered'
        'when the Triage has been Completed'.
  ENDIF.


  IF  zim_in_claim-stat        IS INITIAL AND
      zim_in_pre88-ass_status   = 'T'.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_claim
             WHERE pernr      = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident
               AND claim_type = w_claim_type.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'The Claims Status must be entered'
          'when the Triage has been Completed'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-claim_class IS INITIAL OR
     zim_in_pre88-claim_class EQ 'P' .
    CLEAR zim_in_pre88-parent_incident.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-parent_incident = zim_in_pre88-incident.
    CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'The Parent Incident cannot be the same'
        'Incident Number'.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_2201

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3608
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3608 .

  IF  zim_in_pre88-rec_comp       IS NOT INITIAL AND
      zim_in_pre88-recommendation IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'The Letter cannot be Completed until'
        'a Recommendation has been entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF  zim_in_pre88-rec_comp       IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc
           WHERE pernr        = zim_in_pre88-pernr
             AND incident     = zim_in_pre88-incident
             AND doc_unique   = 'PRE88REC001'.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'The Letter cannot be Completed until'
          'the Recommendation Letter has been generated'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc
       WHERE pernr        = zim_in_pre88-pernr
         AND incident     = zim_in_pre88-incident
         AND doc_unique   = 'PRE88ACC001'.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR : w_code2, sy-ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'The Letter cannot be Completed until'
          'the Notice of Liability Letter has been generated'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_comp    IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_tl IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_ucomm.
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Team Leader cannot complete the'
                      'Review until the Certificate has'
                      'been completed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_comp    IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_mgr IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_ucomm.
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Manager cannot complete the'
                      'Review until the Certificate has'
                      'been completed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_tl  IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_mgr IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_ucomm.
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Manager cannot complete the'
                      'Review until the Team Leader has'
                      'completed their Review'.
  ENDIF.
  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_comp    IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_ucomm.
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Director cannot complete the'
                      'Review until the Certificate has'
                      'been completed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_tl  IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_ucomm.
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Director cannot complete the'
                      'Review until the Team Leader has'
                      'completed their Review'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_mgr IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR : w_ucomm.
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Director cannot complete the'
                      'Review until the Manager has'
                      'completed their Review'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3608

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  validate_3101
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_full_case_management.
  DATA: lv_full_case    TYPE boole_d.

  PERFORM check_full_case_management CHANGING lv_full_case.
* External provider - Full case management mandatory as per the checkbox
  IF zim_in_mast-full_case_mgmt = abap_true AND
    lv_full_case IS INITIAL.        "full case managed
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Full Case Management must'
                      'be created in Service Request'.
  ENDIF.

* set the full case management if 'Full case managed'
  IF zim_screen_serv_req-prov_activity EQ 'F'.
    zim_in_mast-full_case_mgmt = abap_true.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    "validate_3101
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  check_full_case_management
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_full_case_management CHANGING pv_full_case   TYPE boole_d.
  DATA: lv_prov_activity    TYPE zim_in_prov-prov_activity.
* check if entry for full case management exists
  SELECT SINGLE prov_activity
    FROM zim_in_prov
    INTO lv_prov_activity
    WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
      AND prov_activity = 'F'.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    pv_full_case = abap_true.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR pv_full_case.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "check_full_case_management

    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Mobile Number.'
                      'Only enter numbers, do not'

  ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

FORM validate_2103 .

  IF zim_in_mast-mgr_contact IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_mast-mgr_contact GT sy-datum.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employer Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be in the future'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_mast-mgr_contact LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employer Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be before the Date of the Incident'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-employer_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-mgr_contact IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employer Last Contact Date'
                      'must be entered when the Contact'
                      'is complete'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-employer_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-mgr IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employees Commander/Manager'
                      'must be entered when the Contact'
                      'is complete'.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_mgr_name, w_mgr_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-mgr IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-mgr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Commander/Manager'.
    ENDIF.
    w_mgr_name = pa0001-ename.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-mgr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0002.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_mgr_rank.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-mgr
                                CHANGING w_mgr_phone.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_sup_name, w_sup_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-sup IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.
    w_sup_name = pa0001-ename.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0002.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_sup_rank.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup
                                CHANGING w_sup_phone.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_sup_add_name, w_sup_add_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-sup_add IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Additional Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.
    w_sup_add_name = pa0001-ename.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0002.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_sup_add_rank.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup_add
                                CHANGING w_sup_add_phone.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_lam_name, w_lam_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-lam IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-lam
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.

             AND endda GE sy-datum.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_gao_name.
  IF zim_in_mast-gao IS NOT INITIAL.

  CLEAR w_reg_name.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001

             AND endda GE sy-datum.

      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Region HR Manager'.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_2104
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_2104 .

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_contact IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_mast-ins_contact GT sy-datum.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be in the future'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_mast-ins_contact LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be before the Date of the Incident'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-insurer_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_contact IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer Last Contact Date'
                      'must be entered when the Contact'
                      'is complete'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-insurer_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurers First Contact'
                      'must be entered when the Contact'
                      'is complete'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-insurer IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A First Contact must be entered'
                      'when a Second Contact is entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ins_cont
           WHERE insurer = zim_in_mast-insurer
             AND contact = zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid First Contact'.
    ELSE.
      w_ins1_name  = zim_ins_cont-name.
      w_ins1_phone = zim_ins_cont-phone.
      w_ins1_fax   = zim_ins_cont-fax.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR : w_ins1_name, w_ins1_phone, w_ins1_fax.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ins_cont
           WHERE insurer = zim_in_mast-insurer
             AND contact = zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Second Contact'.
    ELSE.
      w_ins2_name  = zim_ins_cont-name.
      w_ins2_phone = zim_ins_cont-phone.
      w_ins2_fax   = zim_ins_cont-fax.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR : w_ins2_name, w_ins2_phone, w_ins2_fax.
  ENDIF.

*  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL AND
*     w_ins-claim_stats IS NOT INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A First Contact must be entered'
*                      'when the Insurer Approval Status'
*                      'is entered'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL AND
*     w_ins-claim_no IS NOT INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A First Contact must be entered'
*                      'when the Insurer Claim Number'
*                      'is entered'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL AND
*     w_ins-claim_sdate IS NOT INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A First Contact must be entered'
*                      'when the Insurer Date Approved'
*                      'is entered'.
*  ENDIF.
*
  IF w_ins-claim_sdate IS INITIAL AND
     w_ins-claim_stats IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date the Claim was Accepted/Rejected'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-imp_provided IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-imp_date IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Date the IMP was provided'
                      'by the Insurer'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-imp_provided IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-imp_date IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Select the IMP Received'
                      'Checkbox if the IMP has been received'.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_2104

*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-mgr
                                CHANGING w_mgr_phone.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_sup_name, w_sup_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-sup IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.
    w_sup_name = pa0001-ename.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0002.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_sup_rank.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup
                                CHANGING w_sup_phone.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_sup_add_name, w_sup_add_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-sup_add IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Additional Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.
    w_sup_add_name = pa0001-ename.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0002.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_sup_add_rank.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup_add
                                CHANGING w_sup_add_phone.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR : w_lam_name, w_lam_rank.
  IF zim_in_mast-lam IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-lam
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid PAM/HR Manager'.
    ENDIF.
    w_lam_name = pa0001-ename.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-lam
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0002.
    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                           pa0002-namzu
                     CHANGING w_lam_rank.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-lam
                                CHANGING w_lam_phone.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_gao_name.
  IF zim_in_mast-gao IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-gao
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid GAO'.
    ENDIF.
    w_gao_name = pa0001-ename.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-gao
                                CHANGING w_gao_phone.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_reg_name.
  IF zim_in_mast-reg IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-reg
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Region HR Manager'.
    ENDIF.
    w_reg_name = pa0001-ename.
*  Telephone number
    PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-reg
                                CHANGING w_reg_phone.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_2203

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_2204
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_2204 .

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_contact IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_mast-ins_contact GT sy-datum.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be in the future'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_mast-ins_contact LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be before the Date of the Incident'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-insurer_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_contact IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer Last Contact Date'
                      'must be entered when the Contact'
                      'is complete'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-insurer_comp IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurers First Contact'
                      'must be entered when the Contact'
                      'is complete'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-insurer IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Insurer must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS INITIAL AND
     zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A First Contact must be entered'
                      'when a Second Contact is entered'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ins_cont
           WHERE insurer = zim_in_mast-insurer
             AND contact = zim_in_mast-ins_first_cont.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid First Contact'.
    ELSE.
      w_ins1_name  = zim_ins_cont-name.
      w_ins1_phone = zim_ins_cont-phone.
      w_ins1_fax   = zim_ins_cont-fax.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR : w_ins1_name, w_ins1_phone, w_ins1_fax.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ins_cont
           WHERE insurer = zim_in_mast-insurer
             AND contact = zim_in_mast-ins_sec_cont.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Second Contact'.
    ELSE.
      w_ins2_name  = zim_ins_cont-name.
      w_ins2_phone = zim_ins_cont-phone.
      w_ins2_fax   = zim_ins_cont-fax.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR : w_ins2_name, w_ins2_phone, w_ins2_fax.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_2204

  IF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL.

           WHERE ima = w_ima.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Injury Management Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = w_ima
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Injury Management Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_ima_name_old = pa0001-ename.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_ima_name_old.
  ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3103
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3103 .

  CLEAR : w_orgeh_name.

  IF zim_screen_rehab-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM hrp1000
           WHERE plvar = c_plvar_01
             AND otype = c_otype_o
             AND objid = zim_screen_rehab-orgeh
             AND istat = c_istat_1
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum
             AND langu = 'E'.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Org Unit'.
    ELSE.
      w_orgeh_name = hrp1000-stext.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_rehab-endda LT zim_screen_rehab-begda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Completion Date cannot be'
                        'less than the Commencement Date'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_rehab-review_date IS INITIAL.
      zim_screen_rehab-review_date = zim_screen_rehab-endda - 7.
      IF zim_screen_rehab-review_date LT zim_screen_rehab-begda.
        zim_screen_rehab-review_date = zim_screen_rehab-begda.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_rehab-review_date LT zim_screen_rehab-begda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Review Date cannot be'
                        'less than the Commencement Date'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3103

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3401
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3401 .

  CLEAR : w_wc_name, w_ip_name, w_si_name.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
  IF w_epss = abap_true.
    PERFORM calculate_epss_ip_dates.
  ELSE.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
    PERFORM calculate_ip_dates.
  ENDIF.

*  IF zim_in_cm-disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
*     zim_in_cm-disablement_date LT zim_incident-dateincident.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date of Disablement cannot be'
*                      'before the incident date'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_in_cm-disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
*     zim_in_cm-disablement_date LT '20120119'.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date of Disablement cannot be'
*                      'less than 19/01/2012'.
*  ENDIF.



  IF zim_in_cm-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Workers Compensation Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Workers Compensation Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_wc_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-ip_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-ip_advisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Income Protection Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-ip_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Income Protection Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_ip_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-claim_review IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-claim_review_dt IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Claim Review Date'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C'.
    IF zim_in_cm-wc_close_date IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the WC Date Closed'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-wc_close_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C' AND
     w_code2 = 'SAVE'.
*   Info type 14 check for delimiting
    PERFORM validate_3415a  USING zim_incident-dateincident
                                  zim_in_cm-wc_close_date.
  ENDIF.


*  IF zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'C'.
*    IF zim_in_cm-ip_close_date IS INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the IP Date Closed'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ELSE.
*    CLEAR zim_in_cm-ip_close_date.
*  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-recur IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-recur_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'USEPLAN'.
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
      PERFORM determine_planned_hours.
      w_planned_hours = w_ip_det-plan_hours.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : w_planned_hours, zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
      IF zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the date the Planned'
                          'Hours should commence from'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* External claims validations must be done when ubsworn claim
  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim  = abap_true.
    PERFORM unsworn_validations.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR: w_ecp_det, w_prim_det.
  ENDIF.

* Adjust the Unsworn Claim indicator.
  w_unsworn_claim = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim.

**<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
** Only on-duty determination for EPSS claims
*  IF w_epss = abap_true and zim_in_cm-duty_det NE 'O'.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Unable to create EPSS record.'
*                      'Check WC Duty determination.'.
*  ENDIF.
**>>> AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3401

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3501
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3501 .

  CLEAR : w_wc_name, w_epss_sup_name, w_epss_name, w_ip_name, w_si_name.

  PERFORM calculate_epss_ip_dates.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Workers Compensation Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Workers Compensation Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_wc_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Examiner ID'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Examiner ID'.
    ENDIF.

    w_epss_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_epss_sup_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-claim_review IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-claim_review_dt IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Claim Review Date'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C'.
    IF zim_in_cm-wc_close_date IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the WC Date Closed'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-wc_close_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C' AND
     w_code2 = 'SAVE'.
*   Info type 14 check for delimiting
    PERFORM validate_3415a  USING zim_incident-dateincident
                                  zim_in_cm-wc_close_date.
  ENDIF.


*  IF zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'C'.
*    IF zim_in_cm-ip_close_date IS INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the IP Date Closed'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ELSE.
*    CLEAR zim_in_cm-ip_close_date.
*  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-recur IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-recur_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'USEPLAN'.
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
      PERFORM determine_planned_hours.
      w_planned_hours = w_ip_det-plan_hours.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : w_planned_hours, zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
      IF zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the date the Planned'
                          'Hours should commence from'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* External claims validations must be done when ubsworn claim
  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim  = abap_true.
    PERFORM unsworn_validations.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR: w_ecp_det, w_prim_det.
  ENDIF.

* Adjust the Unsworn Claim indicator.
  w_unsworn_claim = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim.

**<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
** Only on-duty determination for EPSS claims
*  IF w_epss = abap_true and zim_in_cm-duty_det NE 'O'.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Unable to create EPSS record.'
*                      'Check WC Duty determination.'.
*  ENDIF.
**>>> AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3501

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4101
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4101 .

  CLEAR : w_wc_name, w_epss_sup_name, w_epss_name, w_ip_name, w_si_name.

  PERFORM calculate_epss_ip_dates.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Workers Compensation Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Workers Compensation Advisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_wc_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Examiner ID'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-epss_examiner_id
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Examiner ID'.
    ENDIF.

    w_epss_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_cm-epss_supervisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid EPSS Supervisor'.
    ENDIF.

    w_epss_sup_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-claim_review IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-claim_review_dt IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Claim Review Date'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C'.
    IF zim_in_cm-wc_close_date IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the WC Date Closed'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-wc_close_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-wc_status = 'C' AND
     w_code2 = 'SAVE'.
*   Info type 14 check for delimiting
    PERFORM validate_3415a  USING zim_incident-dateincident
                                  zim_in_cm-wc_close_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'O'.
    w_claim_type = 'E'. " On duty
  ELSEIF zim_in_cm-duty_det = 'F'.
    w_claim_type = 'F'. " Off duty
  ENDIF.

*  IF zim_in_cm-ip_status = 'C'.
*    IF zim_in_cm-ip_close_date IS INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the IP Date Closed'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ELSE.
*    CLEAR zim_in_cm-ip_close_date.
*  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-recur IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR zim_in_cm-recur_date.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_code2 = 'USEPLAN'.
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
      PERFORM determine_planned_hours.
      w_planned_hours = w_ip_det-plan_hours.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : w_planned_hours, zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    IF zim_in_cm-use_plan_hours = abap_true.
      IF zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the date the Planned'
                          'Hours should commence from'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR : zim_in_cm-plan_hours_date.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* External claims validations must be done when ubsworn claim
  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim  = abap_true.
    PERFORM unsworn_validations.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR: w_ecp_det, w_prim_det.
  ENDIF.

* Adjust the Unsworn Claim indicator.
  w_unsworn_claim = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim.

**<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
** Only on-duty determination for EPSS claims
*  IF w_epss = abap_true and zim_in_cm-duty_det NE 'O'.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Unable to create EPSS record.'
*                      'Check WC Duty determination.'.
*  ENDIF.
**>>> AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4101

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  unsworn_validations
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM unsworn_validations.
* Concurrent External Claim.
  IF w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim EQ space.
    IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim IS NOT INITIAL OR
       w_ecp_det-begda        IS NOT INITIAL OR
       w_ecp_det-endda        IS NOT INITIAL.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_PRIM_DET-CONC_EXT_CLAIM'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Concurrent Ext Claim must be set with Ext %'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* Adjust the Concurrent External Claim dates.
  IF w_prim_det-conc_ext_claim NE w_prim_det_old-conc_ext_claim.
    w_prim_det-begda_cec = sy-datum.
    w_prim_det-endda_cec = '99991231'.
  ENDIF.

* External Claim Percentage.
  IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim IS INITIAL.
    IF w_ecp_det-begda IS NOT INITIAL OR
       w_ecp_det-endda IS NOT INITIAL.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_ECP_DET-PC_EXT_CLAIM'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter an external claim percentage'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim GT '100.0'.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_ECP_DET-PC_EXT_CLAIM'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Percentage cannot be more than 100'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* Adjust the External Claim Percentage dates.
  IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim NE w_ecp_det_old-pc_ext_claim AND
     w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF w_ecp_det-begda IS INITIAL.
      w_ecp_det-begda = sy-datum.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_ecp_det-endda IS INITIAL.
      w_ecp_det-endda = '99991231'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* External Claim Percentage dates.
  IF w_ecp_det-begda IS INITIAL.
    IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim IS NOT INITIAL OR
       w_ecp_det-endda        IS NOT INITIAL.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_ECP_DET-BEGDA'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'External Claim % start date must be entered'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  IF w_ecp_det-endda IS INITIAL.
    IF w_ecp_det-pc_ext_claim IS NOT INITIAL OR
       w_ecp_det-begda        IS NOT INITIAL.
      SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_ECP_DET-ENDDA'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'External Claim % end date must be entered'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  IF w_ecp_det-begda GT w_ecp_det-endda.
    SET CURSOR FIELD 'W_ECP_DET-BEGDA'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Start date must be less than date to'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "unsworn_validations

     zim_in_claim-begda IS NOT INITIAL.

  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_claim-stat IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_claim-begda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_claim-endda IS INITIAL AND
       zim_in_claim-begda IS NOT INITIAL.
      zim_in_claim-endda = '99991231'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_claim-begda GT zim_in_claim-endda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be greater'
                        'than the Date To'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

     zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim EQ space.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3405A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3405a .


  IF w_amount_type = 'C' OR
     w_amount_type = 'M'.
    CLEAR zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings.
    IF zim_in_cm-sect_40_ext IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Court Determination and Maternity Leave'
                        'cannot be entered'
                        'whilst the employee is on'
                        'Section 40 External'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_amount_type = 'E'.
    CLEAR zim_in_rec_amt-amount.
    IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim EQ space.
      IF zim_in_cm-sect_40_ext IS INITIAL AND
         zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings IS NOT INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employee must be on'
                          'Section 40 External before an'
                          'Extermal Earnings can be entered'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_rec_amt-amount       IS NOT INITIAL OR
     zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_rec_amt-begda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_rec_amt-endda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date To must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_rec_amt-begda GT zim_in_rec_amt-endda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be greater'
                        'than the Date To'.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_amount_type = 'E'.
      IF zim_in_rec_amt-begda GT sy-datum.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be in the future'.
      ENDIF.

      IF zim_in_rec_amt-begda LT '20140328'.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be before'
                          'the 28/3/2014'.
      ENDIF.

      IF zim_in_rec_amt-endda GT sy-datum.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date To cannot be in the future'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_amount_type = 'C' AND
     zim_in_rec_amt-begda  IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_rec_amt-amount IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Fixed Amount must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

*  IF w_amount_type = 'M' AND
*     zim_in_rec_amt-begda  IS NOT INITIAL AND
*     zim_in_rec_amt-amount IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Fixed Amount must be entered'.
*  ENDIF.

  IF w_amount_type = 'E' AND
     zim_in_rec_amt-begda  IS NOT INITIAL AND
     zim_in_rec_amt-ext_earnings IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The External Earnings Amount'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_rec_amt
         INTO rec_zim_in_rec_amt
         WHERE pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND begda  LE zim_in_rec_amt-endda
           AND endda  GE zim_in_rec_amt-begda.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH 'This record'
                      'will delimit an existing'
                      'Fixed Amount record'.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3405A
