*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3707
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3707 .

  DATA: l_days             TYPE i.

  IF w_gao_mc_date IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF w_gao_mc_date GT sy-datum.
      CLEAR w_ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Medical Certificate Date'
                        'cannot be in the future'.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_gao_mc_date LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      CLEAR w_ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Medical Certificate Date'
                        'cannot be before the date of'
                        'the incident'.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_gao_mc_status IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR w_ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Status of the Injured Worker'
                        'must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_gao_mc_date_from IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR w_ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_gao_mc_date_from LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      CLEAR w_ucomm.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The From Date'
                        'cannot be before the date of'
                        'the incident'.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_gao_mc_status = 'U' OR
       w_gao_mc_status = 'SD' OR
       w_gao_mc_status = 'SP'.
      IF w_gao_mc_date_to IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_ucomm.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date To must be entered'.
      ENDIF.

      IF w_gao_mc_date_to LT w_gao_mc_date_from.
        CLEAR w_ucomm.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date To cannot be less'
                          'than the Date From'.
      ENDIF.

      l_days = w_gao_mc_date_to - w_gao_mc_date_from.
      IF l_days GT 92.
        CLEAR w_ucomm.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Certificate cannot be for'
                          'more than 3 months'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_gao_mc_status = 'R' OR
       w_gao_mc_status = 'P'.
      CLEAR w_gao_mc_date_to.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3707


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3809A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3809a .


  IF w_s49_qctyp IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-s49_begda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen-s49_endda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date To must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen-s49_begda GT zim_screen-s49_endda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be greater'
                        'than the Date To'.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM determine_standard_numbr USING    zim_in_mast-pernr
                                              w_s49_qctyp
                                              zim_screen-s49_begda
                                              zim_screen-s49_endda
                                     CHANGING zim_screen-s49_std_dur
                                              zim_screen-s49_wc_lve
                                              zim_screen-s49_numbr.

    IF zim_screen-s49_numbr GT zim_screen-s49_quota.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Not enough quota for the'
                        'requested leave'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3809A

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3909A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3909a .

  DATA: l_days               TYPE i,
         l_hours             TYPE pa0573-stdaz,
         l_rem               TYPE pa0573-stdaz,
         lv_ip_start         TYPE datum,
         lv_ip_end           TYPE datum.

  CLEAR w_quota_msg.

  IF w_s49_qctyp IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-ipm_pabrj IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Payroll Period and'
                        'Year must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen-ipm_pabrp IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Payroll Period and'
                        'Year must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

*** CR1869 - Allow IP Make-up leave for previous periods
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM t549q
           WHERE permo = '04'
             AND pabrj = zim_screen-ipm_pabrj
             AND pabrp = zim_screen-ipm_pabrp.
*             AND endda GE zim_in_cm-day_271.           "CR1869
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Payroll Period'.
    ENDIF.

***  Begin changes CR1869
*   Calc leave balances using last day selected pay period
    PERFORM calculate_quota USING t549q-endda.
*   get IP dates from ZIM-IN_IP table
    PERFORM get_ip_records.
    gr_ip_rec->get_ip_dates( EXPORTING iv_date = t549q-endda
                             IMPORTING ev_begda = lv_ip_start
                                       ev_endda = lv_ip_end ).
    IF lv_ip_start IS INITIAL AND lv_ip_end IS INITIAL.
      zim_screen-ip_start_date  = lv_ip_start.
      zim_screen-ip_end_date    = lv_ip_end.
    ENDIF.
*
*    IF t549q-endda LE sy-datum.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'IP Make-Up cannot be paid'
*                        'retrospectively'.
*    ENDIF.
***  End changes CR1869

    PERFORM get_standard_hours USING    zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        t549q-begda
                                        t549q-endda
                               CHANGING l_days
                                        l_hours.
    IF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDB' OR
       w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDA'.
      zim_screen-s49_std_dur = l_days.
    ELSE.
      zim_screen-s49_std_dur = l_hours.
    ENDIF.
*
    CLEAR zim_screen-s49_proc.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab35 INTO gr_outtab35
         WHERE lve_endda GE t549q-begda
           AND lve_begda LE t549q-endda.
      IF w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDB' OR
         w_s49_qctyp = 'WCDA'.
        ADD gr_outtab35-lve_days     TO   zim_screen-s49_proc.
      ELSE.
        ADD gr_outtab35-lve_hrs      TO   zim_screen-s49_proc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    IF zim_screen-s49_numbr GT zim_screen-s49_quota.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Not enough quota for the'
                        'requested leave'.
    ENDIF.

    l_rem = zim_screen-s49_std_dur - zim_screen-s49_proc.
    IF l_rem LT 0.
      l_rem = 0.
    ENDIF.

***  Begin changes CR1869
    IF zim_screen-s49_numbr GT l_rem.
      w_quota_msg = text-q01.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'IP Make-Up cannot be greater'
*                        'than the standard work duration'.
    ENDIF.
***  End changes CR1869

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3909A


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_5000
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_5000 .

  IF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = w_ima.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Inspection Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = w_ima
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Inspection Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    w_ima_name = pa0001-ename.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_ima_name.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_5000


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_5100
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_5100 .

  IF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = w_ima.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Assigned To Employee'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = w_ima
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Assigned To Employee'.
    ENDIF.

    w_ima_name = pa0001-ename.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_ima_name.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_ima IS INITIAL AND
     zim_screen-ass_to IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter an Assigned To Employee'
                      'and/or an Assigned To Group'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_5100


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_7010
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_7010 .


  IF zim_screen-corr_code IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_ltr_deft
           WHERE sprsl     = 'E'
             AND corr_code = zim_screen-corr_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Correspondence Code'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR zim_ltr_deft.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab14 INTO gr_outtab14
       WHERE corr_code = zim_screen-corr_code.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Correspondence is already selected'.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_7010


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_7020_1
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_7020_1 .

  IF zim_screen-user_code IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-user_cat = 'N'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid NSWPF Employe Number'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'D'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = zim_screen-user_code.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Medical Discharge Employee'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'M'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = zim_screen-user_code.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid IMA/SIMA'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'W'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = zim_screen-user_code.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Contract Services Advisor'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'Y'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = zim_screen-user_code.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Deployment Officer/Manager'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'L'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = zim_screen-user_code.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Legal Contract Services Advisor'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'V'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-fax.
      PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
             WHERE ima = zim_screen-user_code.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Specialist Claims Officer'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'I'.
      PERFORM get_ins_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-fax
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Insurer Contact Code'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'H'.
      PERFORM get_med_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-fax
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Health Professional Code'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'P'.
      PERFORM get_prov_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                            CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                     zim_screen-fax
                                     zim_screen-user_name.
      IF zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid External Provider Code'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_7020_1


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_7020_2
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_7020_2 .

  IF zim_screen-email IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-email NA '@'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The email address must contain'
                        ' the @ character'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_screen-email NA '.'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The email address must contain'
                        ' the . character'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_screen-email CS '@.'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The @ and the . must be separated by at'
                        'least one character in the email address'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_screen-email CS '..'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Two . (Dots) must be separated by at'
                        'least one character in the email address'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-fax CN ' 0123456789'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Fax Number.'
                      'Only enter numbers, do not'
                      'use Parentheses ( or )'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-home IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_screen-home NA '@'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The home email address must contain'
                        ' the @ character'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_screen-home NA '.'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The home email address must contain'
                        ' the . character'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_screen-home CS '@.'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The @ and the . must be separated by at'
                        'least one character in the home email address'.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_screen-home CS '..'.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Two . (Dots) must be separated by at'
                        'least one character in the home email address'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-sms CN ' 0123456789'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid SMS Number.'
                      'Only enter numbers, do not'
                      'use Parentheses ( or )'.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_7020_2

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_7050
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_7050 .

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_location
         INTO w_zp902_location
         WHERE location_code = w_location_code.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR w_zp902_location.
    CLEAR w_popup_ucomm..
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Location Code'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_other_location IS INITIAL AND
     w_zp902_location-other_text IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_popup_ucomm..
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Other Location'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_nature
         INTO w_zp902_nature
         WHERE nature_code = w_nature_code.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR w_popup_ucomm..
    w_error = 'X'.
    CLEAR w_zp902_nature.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Nature Code'.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_other_nature IS INITIAL AND
     w_zp902_nature-other_text IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_popup_ucomm..
    w_error = 'X'.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Other Nature'
                      'must be entered'.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_7050

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  READ_7050
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM read_7050 .

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_location
         INTO w_zp902_location
         WHERE location_code = w_location_code.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR w_zp902_location.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_nature
         INTO w_zp902_nature
         WHERE nature_code = w_nature_code.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR w_zp902_nature.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " READ_7050

  IF  w_pernr IS INITIAL.

  ENDIF.

            'Please enter an Incident Number'.
  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_7060

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FREE_INC_SEL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM free_inc_sel .

  IF NOT inc_sel_container IS INITIAL.
    " destroy tree containers (detroys contained tree control, too)
    CALL METHOD inc_sel_container->free
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR inc_sel_container.
    CLEAR inc_sel_alv.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " FREE_INC_SEL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  P902_TEMPLATE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM p902_template .

  DATA : l_identnr                TYPE zhr_inc_identnr,
         l_bin_size               TYPE i,
         l_pdf_data               TYPE xstring,
         l_pakey                  TYPE pakey,
         tb_act_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_cau_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_whe_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_nat_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_loc_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_fit_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_tre_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t,
         tb_haz_form              TYPE zp902_desc_form_t.


  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
    EXPORTING
      formname           = 'ZP902_INCIDENT'
      variant            = ' '
      direct_call        = ' '
    IMPORTING
      fm_name            = w_formname
    EXCEPTIONS
      no_form            = 1
      no_function_module = 2
      OTHERS             = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
    EXPORTING
      formname           = 'ZP902_EXPOSURE_HAZARD'
      variant            = ' '
      direct_call        = ' '
    IMPORTING
      fm_name            = w_formname_haz
    EXCEPTIONS
      no_form            = 1
      no_function_module = 2
      OTHERS             = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
    EXPORTING
      formname           = 'ZP902_LEGEND'
      variant            = ' '
      direct_call        = ' '
    IMPORTING
      fm_name            = w_formname_legend
    EXCEPTIONS
      no_form            = 1
      no_function_module = 2
      OTHERS             = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
  ENDIF.

  CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>retrieve_smartform_legend
    IMPORTING
      tb_act_form = tb_act_form
      tb_cau_form = tb_cau_form
      tb_whe_form = tb_whe_form
      tb_loc_form = tb_loc_form
      tb_nat_form = tb_nat_form
      tb_fit_form = tb_fit_form
      tb_tre_form = tb_tre_form
      tb_haz_form = tb_haz_form.


  CLEAR : w_control_parameters, w_output_options.
  w_control_parameters-preview    = abap_true.
  w_control_parameters-no_dialog  = abap_true.
* w_output_options-tddest         = 'LOCL'.
  w_output_options-tdimmed        = abap_true.
  w_output_options-tddelete       = abap_true.

  w_control_parameters-getotf     = 'X'.

  CLEAR w_incident_form.
  CLEAR lt_longt.
  CLEAR tb_injury_form.

  WRITE l_identnr TO w_incident_form-identnr NO-ZERO.
  CONDENSE w_incident_form-identnr NO-GAPS.

  CLEAR w_injury_form.
  APPEND w_injury_form TO tb_injury_form.
  APPEND w_injury_form TO tb_injury_form.
  APPEND w_injury_form TO tb_injury_form.
  APPEND w_injury_form TO tb_injury_form.
  APPEND w_injury_form TO tb_injury_form.
  APPEND w_injury_form TO tb_injury_form.

* Set the parameter so the Smartform file does not close
  w_control_parameters-no_open   = space .
  w_control_parameters-no_close  = 'X' .

  CALL FUNCTION w_formname
    EXPORTING
      control_parameters = w_control_parameters
      output_options     = w_output_options
      lpages_all         = 3
      incident           = w_incident_form
      longtext           = lt_longt
      tb_injury          = tb_injury_form
    IMPORTING
      job_output_info    = w_output_info.

* Set the form to start on the DETAISL page (excludes the header)
  w_control_parameters-no_open   = 'X' .
  w_control_parameters-no_close  = 'X' .
  w_control_parameters-startpage = 'DETAILS'.

  CALL FUNCTION w_formname_haz
    EXPORTING
      control_parameters = w_control_parameters
      output_options     = w_output_options
      lpages_all         = 3
      lpages_offset      = 2
      standalone         = ' '
      p9004key           = l_pakey
    IMPORTING
      job_output_info    = w_output_info.

* Set the parameter so the Smartform file now closes
  w_control_parameters-no_open   = 'X' .
  w_control_parameters-no_close  = space .
  CLEAR w_control_parameters-startpage.

  CALL FUNCTION w_formname_legend
    EXPORTING
      control_parameters = w_control_parameters
      output_options     = w_output_options
      tb_activities      = tb_act_form
      tb_cause           = tb_cau_form
      tb_where           = tb_whe_form
      tb_nature          = tb_nat_form
      tb_location        = tb_loc_form
      tb_currfit         = tb_fit_form
      tb_treatopt        = tb_tre_form
      tb_hazard          = tb_haz_form
    IMPORTING
      job_output_info    = w_output_info.


  PERFORM convert_smartform_to_pdf.

  CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>execute
    EXPORTING
      document               = w_filename
      synchronous            = 'X'
      operation              = 'OPEN'
    EXCEPTIONS
      cntl_error             = 1
      error_no_gui           = 2
      bad_parameter          = 3
      file_not_found         = 4
      path_not_found         = 5
      file_extension_unknown = 6
      error_execute_failed   = 7
      synchronous_failed     = 8
      not_supported_by_gui   = 9
      OTHERS                 = 10.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0 AND
     sy-subrc <> 8.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Error displaying attachment'.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " P902_TEMPLATE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_1000
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_1000 .

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab1, gt_outtab2, gt_outtxt1, gt_outtab58.
  CLEAR   : p9001, gr_outtab1, gr_outtab2, pa0001,
            w_code2, w_1000_changed, gr_outtab58,
            w_inc_act, w_inc_cause, w_inc_where, w_exit,
            zim_inc_p902.

  w_1000_action       = 'INS'.
  w_no_pernr          = abap_true.
  p9001-fitnessstat   = '10'.
  p9001-treatmentopt  = '10'.
  p9001-bp_number     = 'BP1'.

  g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab5.
  CALL SCREEN 1000.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_1000

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_1000_A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_1000_a USING p_pernr
                       p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab1, gt_outtab2, gt_outtxt1, gt_outtab58.
  CLEAR   : p9001, gr_outtab1, gr_outtab2, pa0001,
            w_code2, w_no_pernr, w_1000_changed, gr_outtab58,
            w_inc_act, w_inc_cause, w_inc_where, w_exit,
            zim_inc_p902.

  IF  w_1000_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  PERFORM retrieve_incident USING p_pernr
                                  p_incident.

  IF w_1000_action = 'COP'.
    p9001-nearmiss  = '4'.
    CLEAR p9001-identnr.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-bp_number IS INITIAL.
    g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab1.
  ELSE.
    g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab5.
  ENDIF.

  SET SCREEN 1000.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                  " CALL_1000_A

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_1000_B
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_1000_b USING p_pernr
                       p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab1, gt_outtab2, gt_outtxt1, gt_outtab58.
  CLEAR   : p9001, gr_outtab1, gr_outtab2, pa0001,
            w_code2, w_no_pernr, w_1000_changed, gr_outtab58,
            w_inc_act, w_inc_cause, w_inc_where, w_exit,
            zim_inc_p902.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  PERFORM retrieve_nearmiss USING p_pernr
                                  p_incident.

  IF  w_1000_action  = 'UNM'.
    p9001-nearmiss = '1'.
    CLEAR p9001-treatmentopt.
  ELSEIF  w_1000_action  = 'HIS'.
    p9001-nearmiss = '6'.
  ENDIF.

  IF p9001-bp_number IS INITIAL.
    g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab1.
  ELSE.
    g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab5.
  ENDIF.
  SET SCREEN 1000.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                  " CALL_1000_B

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_2100
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_2100 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5, gt_outtxt6,
            gt_outtab8, gt_outtab10.
  CLEAR   : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5, gt_outtxt6,
            w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name, w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, w_susp_msg, w_2100_changed,
            w_request, w_doc_unique, w_mgr_rank, w_sup_rank, w_sup_add_rank,
            w_lam_rank, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit,  w_sup_phone, w_sup_add_phone,
            w_mgr_phone, w_lam_phone, w_gao_phone, w_reg_phone.

  IF  w_2100_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-mgr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_mgr_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_sup_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_sup_add_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-lam
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_lam_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.
* get telephone details for employer screen
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-mgr
                            CHANGING w_mgr_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-lam
                            CHANGING w_lam_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup
                            CHANGING w_sup_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup_add
                            CHANGING w_sup_add_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-gao
                            CHANGING w_gao_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-reg
                            CHANGING w_reg_phone.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2
                           gt_outtxt2_old
                    USING  'EMP'
                           w_2100_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt3
                           gt_outtxt3_old
                    USING  'MGR'
                           w_2100_action.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2a
                             gt_outtxt2a_old
                      USING  'PRE88EMP'
                             'DISP'.

    PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2c
                             gt_outtxt2c_old
                      USING  'PRE88MGR'
                             'DISP'.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt4
                           gt_outtxt4_old
                    USING  'INS'
                           w_2100_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt5
                           gt_outtxt5_old
                    USING  'NTD'
                           w_2100_action.

  PERFORM determine_unsworn_claim USING p_pernr
                                        p_incident.

  IF g_user_group NE 'V'.
    g_init_ass-pressed_tab = c_init_ass-tab1.
  ELSE.
    g_init_ass-pressed_tab = c_init_ass-tab6.
  ENDIF.

  SET SCREEN 2100.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_2100

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_2100_A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_2100_a USING p_pernr
                       p_incident.


  REFRESH : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5.
  CLEAR   : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5,
            w_code2, zim_incident, w_susp_msg, w_hod_msg,
            rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit, w_2100_changed, w_sup_phone,
            w_mgr_phone, w_lam_phone, w_gao_phone, w_reg_phone.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2
                           gt_outtxt2_old
                    USING  'EMP'
                           w_2100_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt3
                           gt_outtxt3_old
                    USING  'MGR'
                           w_2100_action.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2a
                             gt_outtxt2a_old
                      USING  'PRE88EMP'
                             'DISP'.

    PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2c
                             gt_outtxt2c_old
                      USING  'PRE88MGR'
                             'DISP'.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt4
                           gt_outtxt4_old
                    USING  'INS'
                           w_2100_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt5
                           gt_outtxt5_old
                    USING  'NTD'
                           w_2100_action.

  IF g_user_group NE 'V'.
    g_init_ass-pressed_tab = c_init_ass-tab1.
  ELSE.
    g_init_ass-pressed_tab = c_init_ass-tab6.
  ENDIF.

  SET SCREEN 2100.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                 " CALL_2100_A

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_2200
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_2200 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5, gt_outtxt6,
            gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2c.
  CLEAR   : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5, gt_outtxt6,
            w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name, w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, w_susp_msg, w_2200_changed,
            w_request, w_doc_unique, w_mgr_rank, w_sup_rank, w_sup_add_rank,
            w_lam_rank, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit,  w_sup_phone, w_sup_add_phone,
            w_mgr_phone, w_lam_phone, w_gao_phone, w_reg_phone,
            gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2c, w_main_injury.

  IF  w_2200_action NE 'DISP'.
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>lock_in_pre88
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr    = p_pernr
        i_incident = p_incident
      IMPORTING
        e_locked   = w_locked
        e_name     = w_lock_msg.
    IF w_locked IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The record is currently locked by'
                        w_lock_msg.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_pre88
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_pre88.
    zim_in_pre88-pernr    = p_pernr.
    zim_in_pre88-incident = p_incident.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.

    w_ima_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-mgr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_mgr_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_sup_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_sup_add_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-lam
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_lam_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.
* get telephone details for employer screen
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-mgr
                            CHANGING w_mgr_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-lam
                            CHANGING w_lam_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup
                            CHANGING w_sup_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup_add
                            CHANGING w_sup_add_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-gao
                            CHANGING w_gao_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-reg
                            CHANGING w_reg_phone.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2
                           gt_outtxt2_old
                    USING  'EMP'
                           'DISP'.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt3
                           gt_outtxt3_old
                    USING  'MGR'
                           'DISP'.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2a
                           gt_outtxt2a_old
                    USING  'PRE88EMP'
                           w_2200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2c
                           gt_outtxt2c_old
                    USING  'PRE88MGR'
                           w_2200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt4
                           gt_outtxt4_old
                    USING  'PRE88INS'
                           w_2200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt5
                           gt_outtxt5_old
                    USING  'PRE88NTD'
                           w_2200_action.

  w_claim_type = 'H'.

  g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab1.

  SET SCREEN 2200.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_2200

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_2200_A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_2200_a USING p_pernr
                       p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5, gt_outtxt6,
            gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2c.
  CLEAR   : gt_outtxt2, gt_outtxt3, gt_outtxt4, gt_outtxt5, gt_outtxt6,
            w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name, w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, w_susp_msg, w_2200_changed,
            w_request, w_doc_unique, w_mgr_rank, w_sup_rank, w_sup_add_rank,
            w_lam_rank, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit,  w_sup_phone, w_sup_add_phone,
            w_mgr_phone, w_lam_phone, w_gao_phone, w_reg_phone,
            gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2c.



  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-mgr
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_mgr_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_sup_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-sup_add
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_sup_add_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_mast-lam
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR pa0002.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                         pa0002-namzu
                   CHANGING w_lam_rank.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.
* get telephone details for employer screen
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-mgr
                            CHANGING w_mgr_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-lam
                            CHANGING w_lam_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup
                            CHANGING w_sup_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-sup_add
                            CHANGING w_sup_add_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-gao
                            CHANGING w_gao_phone.
  PERFORM get_telephone_no    USING  zim_in_mast-reg
                            CHANGING w_reg_phone.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2
                           gt_outtxt2_old
                    USING  'EMP'
                           'DISP'.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt3
                           gt_outtxt3_old
                    USING  'MGR'
                           'DISP'.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2a
                           gt_outtxt2a_old
                    USING  'PRE88EMP'
                           w_2200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt2c
                           gt_outtxt2c_old
                    USING  'PRE88MGR'
                           w_2200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt4
                           gt_outtxt4_old
                    USING  'PRE88INS'
                           w_2200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt5
                           gt_outtxt5_old
                    USING  'PRE88NTD'
                           w_2200_action.

  w_claim_type = 'H'.

  g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab1.

  SET SCREEN 2200.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                               " CALL_2200_A

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3011
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3011 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab16.
  CLEAR   : gr_outtab16, w_code2,
            zim_in_mast, zim_incident, w_susp_msg, w_emp_name,
            w_hod_msg, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  IF  sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR' OR
      sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SIMA'.
    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_all_notes USING p_pernr
                              p_incident.

  CALL SCREEN 3011.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3011

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3012
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3012 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab26.
  CLEAR   : gr_outtab26, w_code2,
            zim_in_mast, zim_incident, w_susp_msg, w_emp_name,
            w_hod_msg, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_document_overview.

  CALL SCREEN 3012.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3012

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3100
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3100 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_3300_action, w_exit,
            w_3100_changed, w_mc_updated.

  w_box_text = 'Comments'.
  w_3400_action = 'DISP'.

  IF  w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
  ENDIF.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'CASE'
                           'DISP'.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
                                 p_incident.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_WC' OR
     sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_MC'.
    g_case_act-pressed_tab = c_case_act-tab7.
  ELSE.
    IF g_user_group = 'V'.
      g_case_act-pressed_tab = c_case_act-tab6.
    ELSE.
      g_case_act-pressed_tab = c_case_act-tab1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SET SCREEN 3100.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3100

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3100_A
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3100_a USING p_pernr
                       p_incident.

  g_case_act-pressed_tab = c_case_act-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 3100.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                  " CALL_3100_A

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3100_B
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3100_b USING p_pernr
                       p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8.
  CLEAR :   w_code2,
            zim_prov, w_request, w_3100_changed,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_3300_action, w_exit.

  w_box_text = 'Comments'.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'CASE'
                           'DISP'.

  g_case_act-pressed_tab = c_case_act-tab1.

  SET SCREEN 3100.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                  " CALL_3100_B

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3200
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3200 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, w_3200_changed, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit.

  w_box_text = 'Comments'.

  IF  w_3200_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.


  PERFORM get_ima_name.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt4
                           gt_outtxt4_old
                    USING  'INS'
                           w_3200_action.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt9
                           gt_outtxt9_old
                    USING  'LEGAL'
                           ' '.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'CASE'
                           'DISP'.

  PERFORM determine_unsworn_claim USING p_pernr
                                        p_incident.

  g_case_leg-pressed_tab = c_case_leg-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 3200.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3200

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3300

*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3300 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_in_mast, zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, w_3300_changed, w_3304_refresh, w_ftc_name,
            rec_zim_in_rehab, w_exit, w_imep_exists, w_4001_msg,
            w_persk.

  w_box_text = 'Comments'.

  IF  w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'CASE'
                           'DISP'.

  PERFORM get_depmsg USING p_pernr.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_imep
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    w_imep_exists = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_deploy
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_deploy.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM determine_unsworn_claim USING p_pernr
                                        p_incident.

  g_case_clo-pressed_tab = c_case_clo-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 3300.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3300

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3400
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3400 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8, gt_outtab30,
            gt_outtab29, gt_outtab32, gt_outtab33, gt_outtab33_old,
            gt_outtab3415, gt_outtab3416, gt_outtab3417.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_3300_action, w_exit,
            w_3400_changed, zim_in_claim, w_claim_type, w_3100_action,
            zim_in_rec_amt, w_amount_type, w_gao_mc_status,
            w_3408_refresh, w_piawe_det, w_epb_det, gv_ux_3415,
            zim_in_prim, zim_in_pawe, zim_in_ip, w_emp_ben, w_record_type, w_wkly_pawe.

* ALV to be refreshed
  w_3413_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3415_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3416_refresh = abap_true.
  w_3417_refresh = abap_true.

  w_box_text = 'Comments'.
  w_claim_type = 'W'.

  IF  w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>lock_in_cm
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr    = p_pernr
        i_incident = p_incident
      IMPORTING
        e_locked   = w_locked
        e_name     = w_lock_msg.
    IF w_locked IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The record is currently locked by'
                        w_lock_msg.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
  ENDIF.


  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS Phase 4
* W_EPSS flag was not being set when certificate was updated via WC screen.
  CLEAR w_epss.
  IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_false. " EPSS not applicable for Pre88 Police Employees
    PERFORM determine_if_epss USING gr_outtab7-pernr gr_outtab7-incident.
  ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS Phase 4

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'WC'
                           'DISP'.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
                                 p_incident.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.
  ENDIF.

* PIAWE details.
  PERFORM get_piawe_detail_and_history.

* Entitlement Period Bucket.
  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM get_epb_detail.
  ENDIF.

* Concurrent Claim details.
  PERFORM get_concurrent_details.

* External Claim Percentage.
  PERFORM get_ecp_detail_and_history.

* perform get_reduced_pay.

  g_case_mgt-pressed_tab = c_case_mgt-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 3400.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3400

FORM call_3500 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.



  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8, gt_outtab30,
            gt_outtab29, gt_outtab32, gt_outtab33, gt_outtab33_old.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_3300_action, w_exit,
            w_3500_changed, zim_in_claim, w_claim_type, w_3100_action,
            w_3400_changed, w_gao_mc_status,
            zim_in_rec_amt, w_amount_type,
            w_3408_refresh.

  w_3417_refresh = abap_true.
  w_box_text = 'Comments'.
  w_claim_type = 'I'.

  IF  w_3500_action NE 'DISP'.
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>lock_in_cm
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr    = p_pernr
        i_incident = p_incident
      IMPORTING
        e_locked   = w_locked
        e_name     = w_lock_msg.
    IF w_locked IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The record is currently locked by'
                        w_lock_msg.
    ENDIF.
*    PERFORM lock_im_pernr USING p_pernr.
*    w_3400_action = w_3500_action.
  ELSE.
    w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
    w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
  ENDIF.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
* Check if EPSS
  CLEAR w_epss.
  IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_false. " EPSS not applicable for Pre88 Police Employees
    PERFORM determine_if_epss USING gr_outtab7-pernr gr_outtab7-incident.
  ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Income Protection is not valid'
                          'for Pre 88 Police Officers'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'IP'
                           'DISP'.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
                                 p_incident.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Income Protection is only valid'
                          'for Police Officers'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
* Throw an information message when user hit Income protection button but
* the incident is EPSS.
  IF w_epss = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'This is an EPSS claim!'
                          'Claim details only available via the EPSS buttons.'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
* perform get_reduced_pay.

  g_case_ip-pressed_tab = c_case_ip-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 3500.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3500

FORM call_4100 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.


  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8, gt_outtab30,
            gt_outtab29, gt_outtab32, gt_outtab33, gt_outtab33_old.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_3300_action, w_exit,
            w_4100_changed, zim_in_claim, w_claim_type, w_3100_action,
            w_3400_changed, w_gao_mc_status,
            zim_in_rec_amt, w_amount_type,
            w_3408_refresh.

  w_3417_refresh = abap_true.
  w_box_text = 'Comments'.

  IF  w_4100_action NE 'DISP'.
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>lock_in_cm
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr    = p_pernr
        i_incident = p_incident
      IMPORTING
        e_locked   = w_locked
        e_name     = w_lock_msg.
    IF w_locked IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The record is currently locked by'
                        w_lock_msg.
    ENDIF.

  ELSE.
    w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
    w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
  ENDIF.


  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
* Throw an information message when user hit Income protection button but
* the incident is EPSS.
  PERFORM determine_if_epss USING gr_outtab7-pernr gr_outtab7-incident.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'EPSS is not valid'
                          'for Pre 88 Police Officers'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'IP'
                           'DISP'.

  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
                                 p_incident.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim = abap_true.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'EPSS is only valid'
                          'for Police Officers'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_epss = abap_false.
    MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'This is not an EPSS claim!'
                          'Claim details only available via the IP buttons.'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  zim_in_cm-ip_type = 'E'.

* perform get_reduced_pay.

  g_case_epss-pressed_tab = c_case_epss-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 4100.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_4100

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_3600
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_3600 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab8, gt_outtab9, gt_outtab10, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8.
  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab, zim_screen_erg,
            zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_rehab, w_3300_action, w_exit,
            w_3600_changed, w_mc_updated, w_pre_rec_name,
            w_pre_tl_name, w_pre_mgr_name, w_pre_dir_name.

  w_box_text = 'Comments'.
  w_3400_action = 'DISP'.

  IF  w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>lock_in_pre88
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr    = p_pernr
        i_incident = p_incident
      IMPORTING
        e_locked   = w_locked
        e_name     = w_lock_msg.
    IF w_locked IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The record is currently locked by'
                        w_lock_msg.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_pre88
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_pre88.
    zim_in_pre88-pernr    = p_pernr.
    zim_in_pre88-incident = p_incident.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.

    w_ima_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-rec_comp_user IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-rec_comp_user
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.

    w_pre_rec_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-tl_approved IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-tl_approved
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.

    w_pre_tl_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-mgr_approved IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-mgr_approved
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.

    w_pre_mgr_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_pre88-dir_approved IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = zim_in_pre88-dir_approved
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.

    w_pre_dir_name = pa0001-ename.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case
                           gt_outtxt_case_old
                    USING  'PRE88'
                           'DISP'.

  w_claim_type = 'H'.

  g_case_scu-pressed_tab = c_case_scu-tab1.

  SET SCREEN 3600.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3600
