                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

  PERFORM get_health_details USING p_pernr
                                   p_incident.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
                               p_incident.

* PERFORM get_reduced_pay.

  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_gao
                                w_gao_name.

  IF w_gao_task = 'M'.
    g_case_gao-pressed_tab = c_case_gao-tab7.
  ELSE.
    g_case_gao-pressed_tab = c_case_gao-tab2.
  ENDIF.
  SET SCREEN 3700.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_3700

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  call_7030
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_7030.

  CLEAR : w_incl_base.
  w_incl_sec_loading = 'X'.

  CALL SCREEN 7030 STARTING AT 45 14.
  CHECK: w_code2 <> 'CANC'.

  TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ty_s_wt,
        lgart                     TYPE t512w-lgart,
        aklas                     TYPE t512w-aklas,
      END OF ty_s_wt.

  RANGES: r_pernr                 FOR p9001-pernr,
          r_lgart                 FOR t512w-lgart.

  DATA:
    gt_rng_awart_lwop             TYPE RANGE OF awart,
    gt_rng_lgart_base_fbt         TYPE RANGE OF lgart,
    gt_rng_lgart_ot_sp            TYPE RANGE OF lgart,
    gt_wcstat_max                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zwcstat,
    gs_t500l                      TYPE t500l,
    gs_wcstat_max                 TYPE zwcstat,
    lt_wt                         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_s_wt,
    ls_rng_lgart                  LIKE LINE OF gt_rng_lgart_base_fbt,
    ls_wt                         TYPE ty_s_wt,
    lv_evclv                      TYPE t52d4-evclv.


  SELECT lgart aklas
    FROM t512w
    INTO TABLE lt_wt
    WHERE molga EQ zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_molga_aust
      AND endda GE w_endda "fv_date
      AND begda LE w_endda "fv_date
      AND aklas NE space.

  ls_rng_lgart-sign   = 'I'.
  ls_rng_lgart-option = 'EQ'.

  LOOP AT lt_wt INTO ls_wt.
*   Special case - excluded Wage Types if the incident is
*   before 21/10/2018.
    IF w_incl_sec_loading IS INITIAL.
      IF ls_wt-lgart EQ
              zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-shift_loading_sec_min OR
         ls_wt-lgart EQ
              zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-weekend_load_sec_min.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*   Special case - Wage Types included in base & FBT.
    IF ls_wt-lgart EQ
         zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_lgart-reportable_fbt.
      ls_rng_lgart-low = ls_wt-lgart.
      APPEND ls_rng_lgart TO r_lgart.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

*   Get the value for Evaluation Class 18 (two character value).
*
    lv_evclv = ls_wt-aklas+34(2).       " Use EV18
    CASE lv_evclv.
      WHEN zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_evclv-base OR
           zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_evclv-allowances.
        IF w_incl_base IS NOT INITIAL.
          ls_rng_lgart-low = ls_wt-lgart.
          APPEND ls_rng_lgart TO r_lgart.
        ENDIF.

      WHEN zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_evclv-hda OR
           zif_hr_workers_comp_const=>c_evclv-overtime.
        ls_rng_lgart-low = ls_wt-lgart.
        APPEND ls_rng_lgart TO r_lgart.
      WHEN OTHERS.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.



  REFRESH : r_pernr.
  CLEAR : r_pernr.
  r_pernr-low    = w_pernr.
  r_pernr-option = 'EQ'.
  r_pernr-sign   = 'I'.
  APPEND r_pernr.

  SUBMIT h99cwtr0 AND RETURN
         USING SELECTION-SET 'PAWE'
         WITH pnppernr    IN r_pernr
         WITH begd_cal     = w_begda
         WITH endd_cal     = w_endda
         WITH in_view      = 'X'
         WITH for_view     = ' '
         WITH s_lgart     IN r_lgart.


ENDFORM.             "call_7030

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_7030_REMINDER
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_7030_reminder.

  CLEAR : zim_in_rem, w_ass_emp_name.

  CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER'
    EXPORTING
      i_action           = 'I'
      i_pernr            = zim_in_mast-pernr
      i_incident         = zim_in_mast-incident
    EXCEPTIONS
      reminder_not_found = 1
      invalid_action     = 2
      inc_not_found      = 3
      OTHERS             = 4.

  IF sy-subrc = 1.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Reminder'.
  ELSEIF sy-subrc = 2.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Action'.
  ELSEIF sy-subrc = 3.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Incident'.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                                      " CALL_7030_REMINDER

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_7050
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_7050 USING p_mode.

  w_7050_action = p_mode.
  IF w_7050_action = 'INS'.
    CLEAR : w_location_code, w_other_location, w_nature_code,
            w_other_nature, w_main_inj, w_zp902_location,
            w_zp902_nature.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab1 LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines = 0.
      w_main_inj = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    w_location_code  = gr_outtab1-location_code.
    w_other_location = gr_outtab1-other_location.
    w_nature_code    = gr_outtab1-nature_code.
    w_other_nature   = gr_outtab1-other_nature.
    w_main_inj       = gr_outtab1-main_injury.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_location
           INTO w_zp902_location
           WHERE location_code = w_location_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR w_zp902_location.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_nature
           INTO w_zp902_nature
           WHERE nature_code = w_nature_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR w_zp902_nature.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  CALL SCREEN 7050 STARTING AT 30 5.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_7050

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_7080
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_7080 USING p_mode.

  w_7080_action = p_mode.
  IF w_7080_action = 'INS'.
    CLEAR : w_location_bp, w_nature_bp,
            w_other_nature, w_main_inj, w_zp902_location,
            w_zp902_nature.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab58 LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines = 0.
      w_main_inj = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    w_location_bp    = gr_outtab58-bodily_location_subtype.
    w_nature_bp      = gr_outtab58-injury_subtype.
    w_main_inj       = gr_outtab58-main_injury.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_location
           INTO w_zp902_location
           WHERE location_code = w_location_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR w_zp902_location.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zp902_nature
           INTO w_zp902_nature
           WHERE nature_code = w_nature_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR w_zp902_nature.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  CALL SCREEN 7080 STARTING AT 30 5.

ENDFORM.                                                    " CALL_7080

  CLEAR : r_pernr.

  r_pernr-sign   = 'I'.
  APPEND r_pernr.

  SUBMIT zhrdd_gao_incidents AND RETURN
         VIA SELECTION-SCREEN
         WITH s_gao    IN r_pernr.

ENDFORM.                                              "CALL_GAO_REPORT

*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETRIEVE_IN_MAST
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_PERNR  text
*      -->P_INCIDENT  text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM retrieve_in_mast  USING    p_pernr
                                p_incident.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " RETRIEVE_IN_MAST


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_DEPLOY_SEL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_deploy_sel TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEPLOY'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_SENT_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Sent Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEPLOY'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SIGN_REF_RECD'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Received'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
*  ADD 1 TO l_col.
*  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEPLOY'.
*  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_COMPLETE'.
*  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
*  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Complete'.
*  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
*  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
*
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEPLOY'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ADDTL_INFO_REQD'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Add Info Reqd'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEPLOY'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CASE_ACCEPTED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Case Accepted'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_DEPLOY_SEL

*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPREF
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_depref .

  DATA: l_desc(60)         TYPE c.

  REFRESH gt_outtab40.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_deploy
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab40
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  CLEAR : l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab40 INTO gr_outtab40.
    IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab40-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab40-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab40 FROM gr_outtab40.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPREF

                            '    '.

  PERFORM append_tree USING 'MEET'

                            'Meeting Notes'
                            ' '
                            icon_report.

                            'REM'
                            'Reminders/To Do List'
                            abap_true
                            '    '.

  PERFORM append_tree USING 'REM'
                            'REM_1'
                            'Overview'
                            ' '
                            icon_table_settings.

  PERFORM create_report_nodes.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP'.

                              'TABLE'
                              'Table Maintenance'
                              abap_true
                              '    '.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'

                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_2'
                              'Restricted Org Units'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_3'
                              'Contact Action List'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_4'
                              'Insurance Companies'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_5'
                              'Health Professions'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_6'
                              'Provider Services'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_8'
                              'Reminder/To Do Actions'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_9'
                              'Inbound/Outbound Requests'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE'
                              'TABLE_10'
                              'RTWP Goals'
                              ' '
                              icon_change.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'Root'
                              'LETTER'
                              'Letter Maintenance'
                              abap_true ' '.

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'LETTER'
                              'LET01'
                              'Maintain Letters'
                              ' '

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'LETTER'

    PERFORM append_tree USING 'LETTER'

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_DEPREF_EMP
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEP_LST
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_dep_lst.

  REFRESH: gt_outtab40d, tbl_zim_in_mast.

  CLEAR: zim_in_deploy.

  IF zim_screen-deploy_status = ' '.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_deploy
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab40d
      WHERE deploy_status NE 'C'.

  ELSEIF zim_screen-deploy_status = 'O'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_deploy
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab40d
      WHERE deploy_status = 'O' OR
            deploy_status = 'R'.
  ELSEIF zim_screen-deploy_status = 'C'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_deploy
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab40d
      WHERE deploy_status = 'C'.
  ENDIF.


  IF w_dep IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.
      IF gr_outtab40d-case_officer NE w_dep.
        DELETE gt_outtab40d.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_depref_add.

  SORT gt_outtab40d BY case_accepted_dt DESCENDING.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEP_LST

*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEP_PERNR
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_dep_pernr.

  REFRESH: gt_outtab40d, tbl_zim_in_mast.

  IF w_pernr IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
       INTO TABLE tbl_zim_in_mast
       WHERE pernr = w_pernr.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      LOOP AT tbl_zim_in_mast.
        CLEAR gr_outtab40d.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING tbl_zim_in_mast TO gr_outtab40d.

        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_deploy
               WHERE pernr    = tbl_zim_in_mast-pernr
                 AND incident = tbl_zim_in_mast-incident.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_deploy TO gr_outtab40d.
        ENDIF.

        APPEND gr_outtab40d TO gt_outtab40d.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_depref_add.

    SORT gt_outtab40d BY incident DESCENDING.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEP_PERNR

*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPREF_STAT_DESC
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_PERNR  text
*      -->P_INCIDENT  text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_depref_stat_desc  USING     p_stat
                           CHANGING  p_stat_desc.

  IF p_stat = 'R'.
    p_stat_desc = 'Re-Opened'.
  ELSEIF p_stat = 'O'.
    p_stat_desc = 'Open'.
  ELSEIF p_stat = 'M'.
    p_stat_desc = 'Medical Discharge'.
  ELSEIF p_stat = 'C'.
    p_stat_desc = 'Closed'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPREF_STAT_DESC
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPREF_ADD
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_depref_add.

  DATA : l_desc               TYPE val_text,
         l_value              TYPE domvalue_l,
         l_orgeh              TYPE orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc         TYPE stext.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab40d-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab40d-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab40d-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab40d-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab40d-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab40d-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab40d-prim_injury  = zim_in_mast-prim_injury.
      gr_outtab40d-sec_injury   = zim_in_mast-sec_injury.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab40d-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab40d-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab40d-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                             pa0002-namzu
                       CHANGING gr_outtab40d-rank.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab40d-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab40d-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF gr_outtab40d-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab40d-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      ENDIF.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab40d-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0573
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab40d-pernr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum
             AND ( subty = '5000'
              OR   subty = '5010'
              OR   subty = '5100'
              OR   subty = '5110'
              OR   subty = '5502'
              OR   subty = '5503'
              OR   subty = '5504'
              OR   subty = '5505'
              OR   subty = '5506' ).
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab40d-suspended = 'Yes'.
    ENDIF.


    PERFORM get_depref_stat_desc
      USING gr_outtab40d-deploy_status
      CHANGING gr_outtab40d-status_desc.

    MODIFY gt_outtab40d FROM gr_outtab40d.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab40d BY case_officer.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.
    IF gr_outtab40d-case_officer IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab40d-case_officer NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE    pernr  EQ gr_outtab40d-case_officer
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab40d-dep_adv_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab40d FROM gr_outtab40d.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab40d BY ref_complete_dt.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab40d-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab40d-orgeh.

    MODIFY gt_outtab40d FROM gr_outtab40d.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab40d BY prim_injury.
  CLEAR : l_value, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.
    IF gr_outtab40d-prim_injury NE l_value.
      l_value = gr_outtab40d-prim_injury.
      l_desc  = zcl_olims_class=>get_domain_desc(
                          i_domain = 'ZIM_INJURY_TYPE'
                          i_value  = l_value ).
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab40d-prim_inj_desc = l_desc.

    MODIFY gt_outtab40d FROM gr_outtab40d.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab40d BY sec_injury.
  CLEAR : l_value, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.
    IF gr_outtab40d-sec_injury NE l_value.
      l_value = gr_outtab40d-sec_injury.
      l_desc  = zcl_olims_class=>get_domain_desc(
                          i_domain = 'ZIM_INJURY_TYPE'
                          i_value  = l_value ).
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab40d-sec_inj_desc = l_desc.

    MODIFY gt_outtab40d FROM gr_outtab40d.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab40d BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab40d INTO gr_outtab40d.
    IF gr_outtab40d-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab40d-orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab40d-orgeh_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab40d FROM gr_outtab40d.
  ENDLOOP.



ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPREF_ADD
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4000
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4000 .

  IF w_dep IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
           WHERE ima = w_dep.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Deployment Case Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
           WHERE pernr = w_dep
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Deployment Case Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    w_dep_name = pa0001-ename.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR w_dep_name.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4000
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FREE_DEPREF_LST
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM free_depref_lst .

  IF NOT depref_lst_container IS INITIAL.
    " destroy tree containers (detroys contained tree control, too)
    CALL METHOD depref_lst_container->free
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR depref_lst_container.
    CLEAR depref_lst_alv.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " FREE_DEPREF_LST
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALL_4001
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_4001 USING p_pernr
                     p_incident.

  REFRESH : gt_outtxt10_dep, gt_outtxt10_dep_old,
            gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2a_old,
            gt_outtxt2b, gt_outtxt2b_old,
            gt_outtxt2c, gt_outtxt2c_old,
            gt_outtab41, gt_outtab43,
            gt_outtxt11, gt_outtxt11_old, gt_outtab11,
            gt_outtxt10, gt_outtxt10_old,
            gt_outtab43, gt_outtab43_old.

  CLEAR :   w_code2, zim_screen_address, w_ima_name,  w_hod_msg,
            zim_incident, zim_prov, w_request, w_susp_msg,
            zim_screen_depext, zim_screen_meet, rec_zim_in_dep_ext,
            zim_screen_depmtg, zim_screen_deprev,
            w_3300_action, w_exit, w_4001_changed, w_mc_updated,
            w_old_place_typ, w_emp_plans , w_emp_org, w_emp_plans_txt,
            w_emp_org_txt, w_4001_msg.

  IF  w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>lock_in_deploy
      EXPORTING
        i_pernr    = p_pernr
        i_incident = p_incident
      IMPORTING
        e_locked   = w_locked
        e_name     = w_lock_msg.
    IF w_locked IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The record is currently locked by'
                        w_lock_msg.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_in_mast.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_help_fields.

  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
         WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
           AND incident = p_incident.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR zim_incident.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM retrieve_main_location USING    p_pernr
                                            p_incident
                                            zim_incident-main_loc
                                   CHANGING w_main_injury.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_ima_name.

  PERFORM get_emp_details USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_emp_address USING p_pernr.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-mgr
                               CHANGING w_mgr_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup
                               CHANGING w_sup_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-sup_add
                               CHANGING w_sup_add_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-lam
                               CHANGING w_lam_name.

  PERFORM get_employer_details USING    zim_in_mast-gao
                               CHANGING w_gao_name.

*  IF zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
*    PERFORM get_cm_details USING p_pernr
*                                 p_incident.
*  ELSE.
*    CLEAR w_unsworn_claim.
*  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_insurer_details USING p_pernr
                                    p_incident.

  PERFORM get_act_depref USING p_pernr p_incident.

  PERFORM get_proposed_pos.

  CONCATENATE 'DADJ' '001' INTO w_notes_key.
  PERFORM get_notes  TABLES gt_outtxt10
                            gt_outtxt10_old
                     USING  w_notes_key
                            w_notes_action.

  PERFORM get_depext.
  PERFORM get_depmsg USING p_pernr.


  g_case_dep-pressed_tab = c_case_dep-tab1.
  SET SCREEN 4001.
  LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDFORM.                                              " CALL_4001

*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4002
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4002.

  IF zim_in_deploy-case_officer IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: zim_advisor.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
      WHERE ima = zim_in_deploy-case_officer.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Deployment Case Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
         WHERE pernr = zim_in_deploy-case_officer
           AND begda LE sy-datum
           AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Deployment Case Officer'.
    ENDIF.

    w_dep_name = pa0001-ename.

  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'C' AND
     zim_in_deploy-dep_result IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_code2.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Deployment result must be entered'
                      'when the case is Closed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'C' AND
     zim_in_deploy-close_conf IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_code2.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Confirmation that all documentation'
                      'has been loaded must be selected'
                      'when the case is Closed'.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_in_deploy-prov_code IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_prov
           WHERE prov_code = zim_in_deploy-prov_code.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid External Provider'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4002
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4005
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4005 .
  DATA: l_new_dt  TYPE d.

  IF zim_in_deploy-last_contact_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF zim_in_deploy-last_contact_dt GT sy-datum.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employee Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be in the future'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_deploy-last_contact_dt LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Employee Last Contact Date'
                        'cannot be before the Date of the Incident'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* Meeting
  IF zim_in_deploy-first_mtg_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab44_old LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines < 0.
      CALL FUNCTION 'RE_ADD_MONTH_TO_DATE'
        EXPORTING
          months  = 6
          olddate = sy-datum
        IMPORTING
          newdate = l_new_dt.

      IF zim_in_deploy-first_mtg_dt > l_new_dt.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH
          'Initial Meeting Date is greater than 6 months.'.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_in_deploy-case_accepted_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
        IF zim_in_deploy-first_mtg_dt <
          zim_in_deploy-case_accepted_dt.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH
            'Meeting Date should be greater than '
            'Accepted Date.'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_deploy-first_mtg_tm IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'Please enter the Initial Meeting Time.'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen_depmtg-mtg_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab44_old LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines < 0.
      IF zim_screen_depmtg-mtg_dt < zim_in_deploy-first_mtg_dt.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH
*          'Please enter Next Meeting Date greater than the '
*          'the Initial Meeting Date.'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab44_old INTO gr_outtab44_old INDEX 1.
      IF zim_screen_depmtg-mtg_dt < gr_outtab44_old-mtg_dt.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH
*          'Please enter Next Meeting Date greater than the '
*          'the last Meeting Date.'.
      ELSE.
        CALL FUNCTION 'RE_ADD_MONTH_TO_DATE'
          EXPORTING
            months  = 6
            olddate = gr_outtab44_old-mtg_dt
          IMPORTING
            newdate = l_new_dt.

        IF zim_screen_depmtg-mtg_dt > l_new_dt.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH
            'Next Meeting Date is greater than 6 months.'.
        ENDIF.
        IF zim_in_deploy-case_accepted_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
          IF zim_screen_depmtg-mtg_dt <
            zim_in_deploy-case_accepted_dt.
            MESSAGE i999 WITH
              'Meeting Date should be greater than '
              'Accepted Date.'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_depmtg-mtg_tm IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'Please enter the Next Meeting Time.'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* Case Review
  IF zim_in_deploy-mth_case_rev_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab45_old LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines < 0.
      CALL FUNCTION 'RE_ADD_MONTH_TO_DATE'
        EXPORTING
          months  = 6
          olddate = sy-datum
        IMPORTING
          newdate = l_new_dt.

      IF zim_in_deploy-mth_case_rev_dt > l_new_dt.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH
          'Initial Case Review Date is greater than 6 months.'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_deploy-mth_case_rev_tm IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'Please enter the 3 Month Case Review Time.'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen_deprev-review_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab45_old LINES w_lines.
    IF w_lines < 0.
      IF zim_screen_deprev-review_dt < zim_in_deploy-mth_case_rev_dt.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH
*          'Please enter Case Review Date greater than the '
*          'the Initial Case Review Date.'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      READ TABLE gt_outtab45_old INTO gr_outtab45_old INDEX 1.
      IF zim_screen_deprev-review_dt < gr_outtab45_old-review_dt.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH
*          'Please enter Case Review Date greater than the '
*          'the last Case Review Date.'.
      ELSE.
        CALL FUNCTION 'RE_ADD_MONTH_TO_DATE'
          EXPORTING
            months  = 6
            olddate = gr_outtab45_old-review_dt
          IMPORTING
            newdate = l_new_dt.
        IF zim_screen_deprev-review_dt > l_new_dt.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH
            'Next Case Review Date is greater than 6 months.'.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_deprev-review_tm IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'Please enter the Next Case Review Time.'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


*  IF zim_screen_address-stras IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Street 1 Address'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-ort01 IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Suburb/City'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-regio IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the State'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-land1 IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Country'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-postcode IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Postcode'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  PERFORM postal_code_check IN PROGRAM mp000600
*          USING zim_screen_address-land1
*                zim_screen_address-regio
*          CHANGING zim_screen_address-postcode.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-regio = 'NSW'.
*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpostcodes
*           WHERE pstlz = zim_screen_address-postcode
*             AND ort01 = zim_screen_address-ort01.
*    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid City/Postcode combination.'
*                        'Use the Search Help for a list'
*                        'of valid NSW City/Postcodes.'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-home_phone CN ' 0123456789'.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Home Phone Number.'
*                      'Only enter numbers, do not'
*                      'use Parentheses ( or )'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-mobile CN ' 0123456789'.
*    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Invalid Mobile Number.'
*                      'Only enter numbers, do not'
*                      'use Parentheses ( or )'.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  IF zim_screen_address-email IS NOT INITIAL.
*    IF zim_screen_address-email NA '@'.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The email address must contain'
*                        ' the @ character'.
*    ENDIF.
*    IF zim_screen_address-email NA '.'.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The email address must contain'
*                        ' the . character'.
*    ENDIF.
*    IF zim_screen_address-email CS '@.'.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The @ and the . must be separated by at'
*                        'least one character in the email address'.
*    ENDIF.
*    IF zim_screen_address-email CS '..'.
*      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Two . (Dots) must be separated by at'
*                        'least one character in the email address'.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4005

*&-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_PROP_POS
*&-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_prop_pos TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_TP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SEQ_NO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Assessment No.'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 14.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname    = 'PLACEMENT_TYPE_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos      = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext      = 'Placement Type'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 23.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_TP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PLANS'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Position'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 8.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname    = 'PLANS_STEXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos      = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext      = 'Position Name'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 15.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname    = 'ORGEH_STEXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos      = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext      = 'Org Unit'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 15.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_TP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Start Dt'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 10.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_TP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'End Dt'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 10.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname    = 'OUTCOME_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos      = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext      = 'Outcome'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 15.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DISP_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 7.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 7.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_PROP_POS

*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PROPOSED_POS
*&------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*-------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_proposed_pos.

  DATA : l_seq_no(6)        TYPE n,
         l_outcome          TYPE zim_deploy_tp_outcome,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c.

  REFRESH : gt_outtab41.
  CLEAR   : w_next_pos_seq_no.


  SELECT * FROM zim_in_dep_tp
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab41
    WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
    AND   incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab41 BY seq_no.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab41 INTO gr_outtab41.
    PERFORM get_position USING gr_outtab41-plans
                          CHANGING gr_outtab41-plans_stext
                                   gr_outtab41-orgeh
                                   gr_outtab41-orgeh_stext.

    gr_outtab41-upd_icon  = icon_change.
    gr_outtab41-disp_icon = icon_display.

    w_next_pos_seq_no     = gr_outtab41-seq_no.

    MODIFY gt_outtab41 FROM gr_outtab41.

  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab41 BY placement_type.
  CLEAR : l_outcome, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab41 INTO gr_outtab41.
    IF gr_outtab41-placement_type NE l_outcome.
      l_outcome = gr_outtab41-placement_type.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_DEP_TP_TYPE'
                                     l_outcome
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab41-placement_type_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab41 FROM gr_outtab41.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab41 BY tp_outcome.
  CLEAR : l_outcome, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab41 INTO gr_outtab41.
    IF gr_outtab41-tp_outcome NE l_outcome.
      l_outcome = gr_outtab41-tp_outcome.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_DEPLOY_TP_OUTCOME'
                                     l_outcome
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab41-outcome_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab41 FROM gr_outtab41.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab41 BY seq_no ASCENDING.

  ADD 1 TO w_next_pos_seq_no.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PROPOSED_POS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MC1
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mc1.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cert-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cert-status,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab42.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cert
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab42
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab42 BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab42 INTO gr_outtab42.
    IF gr_outtab42-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab42-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-cat_desc = l_desc.
    IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab42-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab42-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-copy_icon  = icon_create_copy.
    IF gr_outtab42-status NE 'P' AND
       gr_outtab42-inactive IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab42-gen_icon   = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-imp_icon   = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'MC' gr_outtab42-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab42-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab42-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab42-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      gr_outtab42-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab42-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab42 FROM gr_outtab42.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab42 BY status.
  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab42 INTO gr_outtab42.
    IF gr_outtab42-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab42-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-status_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab42-cert_type   = 'M'.
    MODIFY gt_outtab42 FROM gr_outtab42.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_MC1
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CAPACITY1
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_capacity1.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cap-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cap-status,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab42a.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cap
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab42a
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab42a BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab42a INTO gr_outtab42.
    IF gr_outtab42-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab42-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-cat_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab42-mc_sent  = gr_outtab42-cap_sent.
    IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab42-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab42-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-copy_icon  = icon_create_copy.
    IF gr_outtab42-status NE 'P' AND
       gr_outtab42-inactive IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab42-gen_icon   = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-imp_icon   = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'CAP' gr_outtab42-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab42-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab42-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab42-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      gr_outtab42-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab42-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab42a FROM gr_outtab42.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab42a BY status.
  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab42a INTO gr_outtab42.
    IF gr_outtab42-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab42-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab42-status_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab42-cert_type   = 'C'.
    MODIFY gt_outtab42a FROM gr_outtab42.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab42a INTO gr_outtab42.
    APPEND gr_outtab42 TO gt_outtab42.
  ENDLOOP.


  CLEAR w_next_mc_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab42 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab42 INTO gr_outtab42.
    w_next_mc_seq_no = gr_outtab42-seq_no.
  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_mc_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab42 BY mc_date   DESCENDING
                      date_from DESCENDING
                      date_to   DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CAPACITY1

  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Inactive'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-no_out        = abap_true.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_WCERT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_4007
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_4007 .

  DATA: l_dt1   TYPE d,
        l_dt2   TYPE d,
        l_n1(3) TYPE n,
        l_n2(3) TYPE n.


  CLEAR : w_orgeh_name.

  IF zim_screen_depext-desc_ext_org IS NOT INITIAL.

    IF zim_screen_depext-end_date LT zim_screen_depext-start_date.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Completion Date cannot be'
                        'less than the Commencement Date'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_depext-review_date IS INITIAL.
      zim_screen_depext-review_date = zim_screen_depext-end_date - 7.
      IF zim_screen_depext-review_date LT
         zim_screen_depext-start_date.
        zim_screen_depext-review_date = zim_screen_depext-start_date.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_depext-review_date LT zim_screen_depext-start_date.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Review Date cannot be'
                        'less than the Commencement Date'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_screen_depext-start_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
       zim_screen_depext-end_date   IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_dt1 = zim_screen_depext-end_date.
      l_dt2 = zim_screen_depext-start_date.
      l_n1 = ( l_dt1 - l_dt2 ) + 1.
      l_n2 = l_n1 / 7.

      IF l_n2 > 12.
        MESSAGE e999
          WITH 'The duration of placement cannot be greater '
               'than 12 weeks.'.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_4007
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_DEPEXT_REQ
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text

FORM build_fieldcat_depext_req TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.

  l_col = 0.

  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_EXT_ORG'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Organisation'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_EXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'START_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Start Date'.
  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_EXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'END_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'End Date'.
  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_EXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REVIEW_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Review Date'.
  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_EXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SAP_UPDATED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'SAP Updated'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_DEP_EXT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COMPLETE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Complete'.
  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Resend Email'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_DEPEXT_REQ
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPEXT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_depext.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  REFRESH gt_outtab43.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_dep_ext
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab43
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab43 BY employ_seqno.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab43 INTO gr_outtab43.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'DEPEX' gr_outtab43-employ_seqno
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab43-desc_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab43-desc_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab43-email_icon = icon_mail.
    MODIFY gt_outtab43 FROM gr_outtab43.
  ENDLOOP.

  gt_outtab43_old[] = gt_outtab43[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPEXT

  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MC_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Cert Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_FROM'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_TO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAP_HOURS1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Hours/Day'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAP_DAYS1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Days/Week'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAT_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Category'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DIAGNOSIS'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Diagnosis'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
*  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
*    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
*  ELSE.
*    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
*  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*  IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
*    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
*    ADD 1 TO l_col.
*    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COPY_ICON'.
*    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
*    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Copy'.
*    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
*    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
*    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
*    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
*  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF ( w_3400_action NE 'DISP'  AND
       w_3400_action IS NOT INITIAL ) AND
     zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.

    IF sy-tcode NE 'ZWC_GAO'.
*      CHECK zim_in_cm-def_claim = abap_true.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'GEN_ICON'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Upd P905'.
      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.

    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.

       sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.

      ADD 1 TO l_col.

      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Sent Date'.

  ENDIF.

  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INACTIVE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPMTG
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_depmtg.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  REFRESH gt_outtab44.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_dep_mtg
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab44
    WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
    AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab44 BY employ_seqno DESCENDING.

  gt_outtab44_old[] = gt_outtab44[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPMTG


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_DEPMTG_TBL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_depmtg_tbl.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab44 INTO gr_outtab44.
    CLEAR: zim_in_dep_mtg.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_dep_mtg
      WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident
      AND employ_seqno = gr_outtab44-employ_seqno.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'Error occurred when selecting ZIM_IN_DEP_MTG'
        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_dep_mtg-mtg_confirm = gr_outtab44-mtg_confirm.
      UPDATE zim_in_dep_mtg.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_DEP_MTG'
          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_depmtg_log USING 'U'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

* PERFORM get_depmtg.
ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_DEPMTG_TBL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_DEPREV_TBL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_deprev_tbl.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab45 INTO gr_outtab45.
    CLEAR: zim_in_dep_rev.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_dep_rev
      WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
      AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident
      AND employ_seqno = gr_outtab45-employ_seqno.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      w_subrc = sy-subrc.
      ROLLBACK WORK.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH
        'Error occurred when selecting ZIM_IN_DEP_REV'
        'Return code ' w_subrc.
    ELSE.
      zim_in_dep_rev-review_confirm = gr_outtab45-review_confirm.
      UPDATE zim_in_dep_rev.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        w_subrc = sy-subrc.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH
          'Error occurred when updating ZIM_IN_DEP_REV'
          'Return code ' w_subrc.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM update_deprev_log USING 'U'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

* PERFORM get_deprev.
ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_DEPREV_TBL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPREV
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_deprev.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  REFRESH gt_outtab45.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_dep_rev
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab45
    WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
    AND incident   = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab45 BY employ_seqno DESCENDING.

  gt_outtab45_old[] = gt_outtab45[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPREV

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DEPMSG
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_depmsg USING l_pernr.
  IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
    w_4001_msg = 'Pre 88 - HOD Officer'.
  ELSE.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
      WHERE pernr = l_pernr
      AND begda LE sy-datum
      AND endda GE sy-datum.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0220.
    ELSE.
      IF pa0220-subty = '9PSS'.
        w_4001_msg = 'Post 88 Officer'.
      ELSE.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
          WHERE pernr = l_pernr
          AND begda LE sy-datum
          AND endda GE sy-datum.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0220.
        ELSE.
          IF pa0001-persk GE 'XA' AND pa0001-persk LE 'XF'.
            w_4001_msg = 'Post 88 Officer'.
          ELSEIF ( pa0001-persk GE 'XG' AND pa0001-persk LE 'XT' ) OR
                   pa0001-persk  = 'XX' OR
* CR3185 DEVK9HRFNJ - MD
                   pa0001-persk  = 'XY'.
            w_4001_msg = 'Civilian Employee'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_DEPMSG

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  UPDATE_CM_INCIDENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM update_cm_incident .
*  DATA: ls_cm TYPE zim_in_cm,
*        ls_cm_log TYPE zim_in_cm_log,
*        ls_outtab7 TYPE gs_outtab_type7.
*  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO ls_outtab7.
*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
*      INTO ls_cm
*        WHERE pernr    = ls_outtab7-pernr
*          AND incident = ls_outtab7-incident.
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      ls_cm-def_claim = ls_outtab7-def_claim.
*      UPDATE zim_in_cm FROM ls_cm.
*      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Error occurred whilst'
*                              'updating ZIM_IN_CM'.
*      ELSE.
*        zim_in_cm = ls_cm.
*        PERFORM update_in_cm_log USING w_action.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " UPDATE_CM_INCIDENT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PRIM_CLAIM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Get Primary claims from db
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_prim_claim .

  DATA ls_incident TYPE zhr_inc_identnr.



* check if existing object is valid
  IF gr_prim_claim IS NOT INITIAL AND
     ( gr_prim_claim->gv_pernr      NE  zim_in_mast-pernr OR
      gr_prim_claim->gv_incident   NE  zim_in_mast-incident ).
**   not valid, invalidate object
    CLEAR gr_prim_claim.
  ENDIF.

* create primary claim object for the incident

  CASE sy-ucomm .

    WHEN 'ALLPRIM'.

      CLEAR gr_prim_claim.

      CLEAR ls_incident.

      IF gr_prim_claim IS INITIAL.
        CREATE OBJECT gr_prim_claim
          EXPORTING
            iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
            iv_incident = ls_incident.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN OTHERS.


      IF gr_prim_claim IS INITIAL.
        CREATE OBJECT gr_prim_claim
          EXPORTING
            iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
            iv_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PRIM_CLAIM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_CLM_PRIM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_clm_prim  CHANGING   p_fieldcat TYPE lvc_t_fcat
                                         p_hist     TYPE boole_d.
  DATA: l_col         TYPE i,
        ls_fieldcat   TYPE lvc_s_fcat,
        lv_no_out     TYPE boole_d,
        lv_disp       TYPE boole_d.     "display mode

  IF p_hist IS  INITIAL.
    lv_no_out = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.

  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INCIDENT'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Incident'(h01).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA9076'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'WCLMN'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Claim No'(h12).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'(h02).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'(h03).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
    IF sy-tcode      NE 'ZWC_GAO' AND
       w_claim_type  =  'W'.
      CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
      ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'(h05).
      ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      ls_fieldcat-no_out        = p_hist.
      ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

      CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
      ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
      ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESCRIPTION'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Description'(h04).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_USER'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Changed By'(h08).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_DATE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Change Date'(h06).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_TIME'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Change Time'(h07).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRIM'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INACTIVE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Record Inactive'(h09).
  ls_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_CLM_PRIM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHECK_SCREEN_3415  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Screen validations
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_screen_3415 INPUT.

  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  w_3400_changed = abap_true.

* screen input check
  PERFORM validate_3415.


*   Check input date range is overwriting other incidents
  PERFORM check_prim_range_overwrite USING zim_in_prim-begda
                                           zim_in_prim-endda.



*   Info type 14 check
  PERFORM validate_3415a  USING zim_in_prim-begda
                                zim_in_prim-endda.

ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_3415  INPUT
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  MODULE check_begda_screen3415 INPUT
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_begda_screen3415 INPUT.
*   check if begda is not too far in future, warning if so
  PERFORM validate_begda USING  zim_in_prim-begda.
ENDMODULE.                    "check_begda_screen3415 INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Screen 3415
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3415 .

  IF zim_in_prim-begda IS NOT INITIAL OR
     zim_in_prim-endda IS NOT INITIAL OR
     zim_in_prim-description IS NOT INITIAL.
*   one of the fields have been entered, perform validations

    IF zim_in_prim-begda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_prim-endda IS INITIAL AND
       zim_in_prim-begda IS NOT INITIAL.
      zim_in_prim-endda = '99991231'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_prim-begda GT zim_in_prim-endda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be greater'
                        'than Date To'.
    ENDIF.

*   Set claim number to default
    IF zim_in_prim-description IS  INITIAL.
      zim_in_prim-description = w_ins-claim_no.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  validate_begda
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Warning: Start date in more than a year in future !
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_begda USING pv_date   TYPE datum.
  DATA: lv_date_365   TYPE datum,
        lv_char10     TYPE char10.

  CHECK pv_date IS  NOT INITIAL.

  lv_date_365 = sy-datum + 365.
  IF pv_date GT lv_date_365.
    MESSAGE w999 WITH 'Start date entered is more than a year '
                      'in future. Please check'.
  ENDIF.

  IF pv_date LT zim_incident-dateincident.
    WRITE zim_incident-dateincident TO lv_char10.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Start date is prior to Date of Incident '
                      lv_char10.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "validate_begda

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  validate_begda_epss_p2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Warning: Start date in more than a year in future !
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_begda_epss_p2 USING pv_date   TYPE datum.
  DATA: lv_date   TYPE datum.

  CHECK pv_date IS  NOT INITIAL.

  lv_date = zim_in_cm-epss_start_date_p2.
  IF pv_date LT lv_date.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'From date entered must be after '
                      'the EPSS Period 2 start date.'
                      'Please check'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "validate_begda_epss_p2
