MODULE user_command_3415 INPUT.
  PERFORM on_user_command_3415.
ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3415  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ON_USER_COMMAND_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM on_user_command_3415 .
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'PRIM'.
      PERFORM ux_3415  USING w_code2.
    WHEN 'ALLPRIM'.
      PERFORM ux_3415  USING w_code2.
    WHEN 'PRIMHIST'.
      PERFORM ux_3415  USING w_code2.
    WHEN 'CLR'.
      CLEAR zim_in_prim.
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.                    " ON_USER_COMMAND_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ux_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->PV_3415    text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ux_3415  USING pv_3415 TYPE syucomm.
  CASE pv_3415.
    WHEN 'PRIM'.
      PERFORM prim_all_alv_3415  USING space
                                       space.
    WHEN 'ALLPRIM'.
      PERFORM prim_all_alv_3415  USING abap_true
                                       space.
    WHEN 'PRIMHIST'.
      PERFORM prim_all_alv_3415  USING abap_true
                                       abap_true.
    WHEN OTHERS.
      PERFORM  fill_alv_outtab3415  USING gr_prim_claim->gt_prim_pernr.
*     Refresh ALV on frontend
      CLEAR w_3415_refresh.
      CALL METHOD clm_prim_alv->get_frontend_layout
        IMPORTING
          es_layout = clm_prim_layout.
      clm_prim_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
      CALL METHOD clm_prim_alv->set_frontend_layout
        EXPORTING
          is_layout = clm_prim_layout.
*     Refresh ALV with Stable refresh
      CALL METHOD clm_prim_alv->refresh_table_display( ).
  ENDCASE.
  gv_ux_3415 =   pv_3415.
ENDFORM.                                                    "ux_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  prim_all_alv_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM prim_all_alv_3415  USING    p_all   TYPE boole_d
                                 p_del   TYPE boole_d.
  DATA: lt_prim  TYPE TABLE OF  zim_in_prim,
        ls_prim  TYPE zim_in_prim.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
        <ls_outtab3415>   TYPE gs_outtab_type3415.

  IF p_del = abap_true.
    lt_prim = gr_prim_claim->get_primary_including_inactive( ).
  ELSEIF p_all = abap_true.
    lt_prim = gr_prim_claim->get_primary_claims_pernr( ).
  ELSE.
    lt_prim = gr_prim_claim->get_primary_claims_incident( ).
  ENDIF.

* Outtab for ALV Display
  PERFORM  fill_alv_outtab3415  USING lt_prim.

  PERFORM build_fieldcat_clm_prim CHANGING clm_prim_fieldcat
                                           p_del.
* change the fieldcatalog
  clm_prim_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog(
    EXPORTING it_fieldcatalog = clm_prim_fieldcat ).

* optimize layout
  clm_prim_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
  CALL METHOD clm_prim_alv->set_frontend_layout
    EXPORTING
      is_layout = clm_prim_layout.

* Refresh ALV with Stable refresh
  CALL METHOD clm_prim_alv->refresh_table_display( ).
ENDFORM.                    " prim_all_alv_3415
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3415A
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       For any new or deleted Primary claim, validate against InfoType
*       14 existing record. If found, send warning mesage
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3415a USING pv_begda  TYPE sydatum
                          pv_endda  TYPE sydatum.

  TYPES:  BEGIN OF lty_pa0014,
            subty   TYPE subty,
            begda   TYPE begda,
            endda   TYPE begda,
          END OF lty_pa0014.

  DATA: lt_pa0014         TYPE TABLE OF lty_pa0014,
        ls_pa0014         TYPE lty_pa0014,
        lv_var            TYPE string,
        ls_bapiret2       TYPE bapiret2,
        lv_count          TYPE n        VALUE 1,
        lv_start          TYPE char10,
        lv_end            TYPE char10.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
        <lv_msg>          TYPE symsgv.

* if date from exists, proceed with validation against IT14
  CHECK pv_begda IS NOT INITIAL.
* Get IT 14 records which are valid on begin date
  SELECT subty begda endda FROM pa0014
     INTO TABLE lt_pa0014
     WHERE pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr
       AND subty   = '9WCO'
       AND ( begda  LE pv_begda OR
             endda  GE pv_begda ).
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    SORT lt_pa0014 BY begda endda.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_pa0014 COMPARING begda endda.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT lt_pa0014 INTO ls_pa0014.
*   Find a record in LIMBO which needs manual adjustment
    IF ( ls_pa0014-begda BETWEEN pv_begda AND pv_endda AND
         ls_pa0014-endda GT pv_endda ) OR
       ( ls_pa0014-endda BETWEEN pv_begda AND pv_endda AND
         ls_pa0014-begda LT pv_begda ).

      lv_count = lv_count + 1.
      IF lv_count LE 4. " Max of 3 MSGV1 MSGV2 and MSGV3
        lv_var = |MESSAGE_V{ lv_count }|.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_var OF STRUCTURE ls_bapiret2 TO <lv_msg>.
        CLEAR: lv_start, lv_end.
        CALL FUNCTION 'FORMAT_DATE_4_OUTPUT'
          EXPORTING
            datin  = ls_pa0014-begda
            format = 'DD.MM.YYYY'
          IMPORTING
            datex  = lv_start.
        CALL FUNCTION 'FORMAT_DATE_4_OUTPUT'
          EXPORTING
            datin  = ls_pa0014-endda
            format = 'DD.MM.YYYY'
          IMPORTING
            datex  = lv_end.
        CONCATENATE ls_pa0014-subty lv_start lv_end INTO <lv_msg>
          SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF ls_bapiret2-message_v2 IS  NOT INITIAL.
    ls_bapiret2-message_v1 =
      |{ lv_count - 1 } P905 Payroll records may need manual adjustment:|.
    MESSAGE i999 WITH ls_bapiret2-message_v1
                      ls_bapiret2-message_v2
                      ls_bapiret2-message_v3
                      ls_bapiret2-message_v4
                      DISPLAY LIKE 'W'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3415A
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_CLM_PAWE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_clm_pawe  USING p_hist     TYPE boole_d
                              CHANGING p_fieldcat TYPE lvc_t_fcat.

  DATA: l_col         TYPE i,
        ls_fieldcat   TYPE lvc_s_fcat,
        lv_no_out     TYPE boole_d.

  IF p_hist IS  INITIAL.
    lv_no_out = abap_true.
  ENDIF.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.

  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INCIDENT'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Incident'(h01).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'(h02).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'(h03).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PERCENT_INC'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Percentage Increase'(h10).
  ls_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MANUAL_ENTRY'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Manual'.
  ls_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


  IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
    ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'(h05).
    ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    ls_fieldcat-no_out        = p_hist.
    ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
    ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
    ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    ls_fieldcat-no_out        = p_hist.
    ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ls_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  ls_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESCRIPTION'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Description'(h04).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_USER'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Changed By'(h08).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_DATE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Change Date'(h06).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_TIME'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Change Time'(h07).
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR ls_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PAWE'.
  ls_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INACTIVE'.
  ls_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  ls_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Record Inactive'(h09).
  ls_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  ls_fieldcat-no_out        = lv_no_out.
  APPEND ls_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_CLM_PAWE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_PAWE_CALC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       PAWE Calculations
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_pawe_calc .
* check if existing object is valid
  IF gr_pawe IS NOT INITIAL AND
     ( gr_pawe->gv_pernr    NE  zim_in_mast-pernr OR
      gr_pawe->gv_incident  NE  zim_in_mast-incident ).
*   not valid, invalidate object
    CLEAR gr_pawe.
  ENDIF.

* create pawe object for the incident
  IF gr_pawe IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT gr_pawe
      EXPORTING
        iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
        iv_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  ENDIF.

  lt_pawe1 = gr_pawe->gt_pawe.

** If no current records for PAWE exist, and in change mode
*  IF gr_pawe->gt_pawe  IS  INITIAL AND
*     w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
*    ls_in_pawe = gr_pawe->calculate_pawe( ).
*    gr_pawe->set_curr_pawe( is_pawe = ls_in_pawe ).
*
**    ls_pawe_upd = ls_in_pawe.
**    APPEND ls_pawe_upd TO lt_pawe_upd.
**    gr_pawe->put_pawe_upd( it_pawe_upd = lt_pawe_upd ).
**    MESSAGE s999 WITH 'PAWE Calculation added'
**                      ' automatically by the system'.
*  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_PAWE_CALC

MODULE check_screen_3416 INPUT.
  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  w_3400_changed = abap_true.

* screen input check
  PERFORM validate_3416.

ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_3416  INPUT

ENDMODULE.                    "check_begda_3416 INPUT
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_begda_4104 INPUT.
  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT' OR
     w_code2 = 'CLR'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

*   check if from date is within Period 2.
  PERFORM validate_begda_epss_p2 USING  zim_in_epss_p2rd-begda.

ENDMODULE.                    "check_begda_4104 INPUT

ENDMODULE.                    "check_begda_3417 INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDATE_3416
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       validate screen 3416
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM validate_3416 .
  IF zim_in_pawe-begda IS NOT INITIAL OR
     zim_in_pawe-description IS NOT INITIAL OR
     zim_in_pawe-percent_inc IS NOT INITIAL.
*   one of the fields have been entered, perform validations
    IF zim_in_pawe-begda IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_pawe-endda IS INITIAL AND
       zim_in_pawe-begda IS NOT INITIAL.
      zim_in_pawe-endda = '99991231'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_pawe-begda GT zim_in_pawe-endda.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From cannot be greater'
                        'than Date To'.
    ENDIF.

    IF zim_in_pawe-percent_inc IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Percentage Increase must be entered'.
    ENDIF.
* calculate new PAWE
    w_wkly_pawe     = w_emp_ben-wkly_pfull_cur *
                                zim_in_pawe-percent_inc.
    zim_in_pawe-manual_entry  = abap_true.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR: zim_in_pawe, w_wkly_pawe.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_3416
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3416  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3416 INPUT.
  PERFORM on_user_command_3416.
ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3416  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ON_USER_COMMAND_3416
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM on_user_command_3416 .
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'PAWEREC'.
      PERFORM pawe_all_alv_3416  USING space.
    WHEN 'PAWEHIST'.
      PERFORM pawe_all_alv_3416  USING abap_true.

    WHEN 'RT_RESULT'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_PAWE_RESULTS'
        EXPORTING
          it_awe_rt = w_emp_ben-it_awe_rt.
    WHEN 'LOST_ALL'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_PAWE_LOST'
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
        CHANGING
          it_lost    = w_emp_ben-it_lost.

      CLEAR w_emp_ben-wkly_lost_all.
      LOOP AT w_emp_ben-it_lost INTO w_lost.
        IF w_lost-excl_allow IS INITIAL.
          ADD w_lost-betrg TO w_emp_ben-wkly_lost_all.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      w_emp_ben-wkly_pfull_cur = w_emp_ben-wkly_full_cur +
                                 w_emp_ben-wkly_lost_all.
      w_emp_ben-wkly_pawe_cur  = w_emp_ben-wkly_pfull_cur *
                                 w_emp_ben-percent_inc.
      IF w_emp_ben-wkly_pawe_cur LT w_emp_ben-wkly_pfull_cur.
        w_emp_ben-wkly_pawe_cur = w_emp_ben-wkly_pfull_cur.
      ENDIF.



    WHEN 'CLR'.
      CLEAR: zim_in_pawe, w_wkly_pawe.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " ON_USER_COMMAND_3416
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PAWE_ALL_ALV_3416
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_SPACE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pawe_all_alv_3416  USING    p_hist   TYPE boole_d.
  DATA: lt_pawe  TYPE TABLE OF  zim_in_pawe,
        ls_pawe  TYPE zim_in_pawe.
  DATA: ls_in_pawe1    TYPE zim_in_pawe.


  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
        <ls_outtab3416>   TYPE gs_outtab_type3416.

  IF p_hist = abap_true.
    lt_pawe = gr_pawe->get_primary_including_inactive( ).
  ELSE.
    lt_pawe = gr_pawe->gt_pawe.
  ENDIF.

  lt_pawe1 = lt_pawe.

  PERFORM fill_outtab_3416.

  PERFORM build_fieldcat_clm_pawe USING p_hist CHANGING clm_pawe_fieldcat.

* change the fieldcatalog
  clm_pawe_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog(
    EXPORTING it_fieldcatalog = clm_pawe_fieldcat ).

* optimize layout
  clm_pawe_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
  CALL METHOD clm_pawe_alv->set_frontend_layout
    EXPORTING
      is_layout = clm_pawe_layout.

* Refresh ALV with Stable refresh
  clm_pawe_alv->refresh_table_display( ).

ENDFORM.                    " PAWE_ALL_ALV_3416
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_PRIM_RANGE_OVERWRITE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_prim_range_overwrite  USING pv_begda  TYPE sydatum
                          pv_endda  TYPE sydatum.


  DATA: lv_var            TYPE string,
        lv_prev           TYPE string,
        ls_bapiret2       TYPE bapiret2,
        lv_count          TYPE n        VALUE 1,
        lv_start          TYPE char10,
        lv_end            TYPE char10,
        lt_prim_dates     TYPE TABLE OF zim_in_prim,
        ls_prim_dates     TYPE zim_in_prim,
        lv_inc_str        TYPE string.


  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
        <lv_msg>          TYPE symsgv.

  gr_prim_claim->get_primary_claims_for_date(
     EXPORTING iv_begda = pv_begda
               iv_endda = pv_endda
     IMPORTING et_prim_claim = lt_prim_dates ).

  LOOP AT lt_prim_dates INTO ls_prim_dates.
    lv_count = lv_count + 1.
    IF lv_count LE 4. " Max of 3 MSGV2 MSGV3 and MSGV4
      lv_var = |MESSAGE_V{ lv_count }|.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_var OF STRUCTURE ls_bapiret2 TO <lv_msg>.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        CLEAR: lv_start, lv_end.
        CALL FUNCTION 'FORMAT_DATE_4_OUTPUT'
          EXPORTING
            datin  = ls_prim_dates-begda
            format = 'DD.MM.YYYY'
          IMPORTING
            datex  = lv_start.
        CALL FUNCTION 'FORMAT_DATE_4_OUTPUT'
          EXPORTING
            datin  = ls_prim_dates-endda
            format = 'DD.MM.YYYY'
          IMPORTING
            datex  = lv_end.
        CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT'
          EXPORTING
            input  = ls_prim_dates-incident
          IMPORTING
            output = lv_inc_str.
        CONCATENATE lv_inc_str lv_start lv_end INTO <lv_msg>
          SEPARATED BY space.

        IF lv_prev IS NOT INITIAL.
          ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_prev OF STRUCTURE ls_bapiret2 TO <lv_msg>.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            CONCATENATE <lv_msg> ' ,' INTO <lv_msg>.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        lv_prev = lv_var.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF lv_count = 5.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_prev OF STRUCTURE ls_bapiret2 TO <lv_msg>.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        CONCATENATE <lv_msg> ' ...' INTO <lv_msg>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF ls_bapiret2-message_v2 IS  NOT INITIAL.
    ls_bapiret2-message_v1 =
      |{ lv_count - 1 } Primary claim date range will be affected:|.
*tvermeulen
    MESSAGE w999 WITH ls_bapiret2-message_v1
                      ls_bapiret2-message_v2
                      ls_bapiret2-message_v3
                      ls_bapiret2-message_v4.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_PRIM_RANGE_OVERWRITE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHECK_DISBDATE_3417  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_disbdate_3417 INPUT.
  DATA: lv_char10   TYPE char10.
* Validations for Date of disablement
  IF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis.
    IF zim_in_ip-disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
       zim_in_ip-disablement_date LT zim_incident-dateincident.
      WRITE zim_incident-dateincident TO lv_char10.
      MESSAGE w999 WITH 'The Date of Disablement cannot be'
                        'before incident date'
                        lv_char10.
    ENDIF.
    IF zim_in_ip-disablement_date IS NOT INITIAL AND
       zim_in_ip-disablement_date LT '20120119'.
      MESSAGE w999 WITH 'The Date of Disablement cannot be'
                        'less than 19/01/2012'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_DISBDATE_3417  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RETRIEVE_MAIN_LOCATION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P_PERNR  text
*      -->P_P_INCIDENT  text
*      <--P_W_MAIN_INJURY  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM retrieve_main_location  USING    p_pernr
                                      p_incident
                                      p_main_loc
                             CHANGING p_main_injury.

  DATA : l_inc_inj          TYPE zim_inc_inj,
         l_p902_inj         TYPE zim_inc_p902_inj.

* Display the original main injury from the 902
  IF p_main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
    CONCATENATE 'LOC_' p_main_loc
                INTO w_fieldname.
    LOOP AT nametab
         WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
      p_main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_inj
           INTO l_inc_inj
           WHERE pernr       = p_pernr
             AND identnr     = p_incident
             AND main_injury = 'X'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT SINGLE location_desc FROM zp902_locationt
             INTO p_main_injury
             WHERE sprsl         = 'E'
               AND location_code = l_inc_inj-location_code.
    ELSE.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_p902_inj
             INTO l_p902_inj
             WHERE pernr       = p_pernr
               AND incident    = p_incident
               AND main_injury = 'X'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SELECT SINGLE body FROM zpbp_body_locatt
               INTO p_main_injury
               WHERE sprsl  = 'E'
                 AND sys_id = l_p902_inj-bodily_location_subtype.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " RETRIEVE_MAIN_LOCATION
